# BABAL WEARS — Complete file-by-file source

This companion contains the complete application source, including vendored JavaScript. The runnable ZIP also includes the two binary font files. Empty Python package markers are intentionally empty. Demo artwork is generated by the included management command; no external image download is needed.

# BABAL WEARS — Make it yours

A complete Django 5.2 + PostgreSQL custom T-shirt studio. Django templates, HTMX, Alpine.js, GSAP and a shared canvas renderer power the experience. No SPA framework, Node build step, Instagram scraping, gateway credentials, real payment account or fabricated customer testimonials are included.

## 1. Brand analysis (assumptions)

The supplied Instagram profile and highlight links could not be fetched. No brand screenshots were supplied with this request. The following are creative assumptions, not observations of BABAL WEARS' actual identity:

| Area | Working assumption |
| --- | --- |
| Voice | Playful, self-aware, direct, with light Nepali/Roman-Nepali accents. |
| Audience | Mobile-first Nepali customers interested in expressive everyday streetwear. |
| Identity | A temporary typographic `babal*` wordmark, warm paper, ink, acidic lime and small coral/cobalt accents. |
| T-shirts | Five placeholder colors: Washed black, Chalk, Moss, Lilac, Burnt orange. |
| Prints | Four original demo artworks: Offline club, Low battery, Own lane, Waveform. These are not Instagram highlight designs. |
| Pricing | Demo base price NPR 1,200, delivery NPR 150, sizes S–XXL. Print/color surcharges default to zero. |
| Other products | Illustrative crewneck, cap, tote and hoodie. Every demo product and price is marked. |
| Payments | COD works immediately. Pay First appears when an admin enables actual payment details. |
| Delivery | Availability and timing are confirmed manually. No specific delivery area or delivery-time promise is invented. |

The tiny woven diamond band is an original geometric reference to Dhaka weaving, not an authenticated textile pattern or a claimed brand asset. No mountains, flags, temples or unrelated cultural symbols are used as decoration.

## 2. Style guide

| Token | Value | Role |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Ink | `#17191B` | Main text, primary buttons, rules |
| Paper | `#F4F1E8` | Main canvas |
| Electric lime | `#D5FF45` | Stickers, typographic interruptions, active accents |
| Cobalt | `#375CFF` | Focus rings, artwork |
| Coral | `#FF785D` | Secondary stickers and artwork |
| Lilac | `#C8BCF2` | Accent surfaces and artwork |
| Muted text | `#555952` | Supporting copy |

Inter variable is the main grotesque; Noto Sans Devanagari handles Nepali. Both are self-hosted WOFF2 files with bundled OFL licenses. Font stacks retain system fallbacks. The base UI scale is 12 / 14 / 16 / 24 / 40 / 72 / 120px, with fluid display headings up to 140px and compact editorial metadata. Content uses a 12-column desktop grid, an 8px spacing rhythm, and 20px mobile page gutters. Desktop columns collapse to one on small screens.

```css
:root {
  --glass-bg: rgba(255, 255, 249, .72);
  --glass-border: rgba(255, 255, 255, .85);
  --glass-shadow: 0 12px 40px rgba(28, 32, 25, .07);
  --glass-blur: 20px;
  --radius: 18px;
}
```

Glass has an opaque fallback. Foreground copy uses dark ink or muted dark text over pale surfaces, and cream over dark buttons. Focus rings are visible. Motion respects `prefers-reduced-motion`. The wardrobe also supports arrow keys, Home/End, swatches and explicit previous/next buttons.

### Five signature interactions

1. **The flowing rail:** pointer drag, touch swipe and wheel movement with velocity-based momentum, center selection and GSAP elastic hanger sway. At rail boundaries, vertical wheel movement returns to page scrolling.
2. **Color as atmosphere:** the background tint morphs with the focused garment; selecting a shirt lifts it from the rail and settles it into the print stage.
3. **The sticker deck:** slightly fanned cards lift into focus; choosing art immediately redraws the canvas. Chest, center and back views share one renderer, with 60–140% scale controls.
4. **The reveal:** a short GSAP spin leads to a final tee. Export produces a 1000×1200 PNG with a view/placement label; Web Share is used where supported and falls back to download.
5. **The numbered journey:** the persistent 01–05 control tracks progress; the final receipt contains an order ID, saved design, price recap, status and Instagram DM link.

## 3. Architecture

| Layer | Responsibility |
| --- | --- |
| Nginx, unprivileged | Entry point, 12 MB request limit, public media, trusted forwarding headers |
| Gunicorn, non-root | Two workers × two threads, WSGI request handling |
| Django + WhiteNoise | Templates, forms, CSRF, static asset compression, admin, order creation and authorization |
| PostgreSQL 16 | Catalogue, configuration, orders, moderation, sessions and shared rate-limit buckets |
| Public media volume | Sanitized print/product/review images and brand-uploaded payment QR images |
| Private media volume | Order design PNGs and payment screenshots; no public media mapping |
| HTMX | Explore category filtering and review-form submission, with ordinary form fallbacks |
| Alpine.js | Payment timing/provider panel state |
| GSAP + vanilla JS | Rail physics, step transitions, reveal, canvas composition, checkout submission |

`studio/services.py` prices orders from database records within a transaction. The browser cannot set price, payment verification or order status. A session-bound submission key makes retries return the same order. Order metadata snapshots preserve color/print names and totals when catalogue entries change.

The generated image is a visual reference only. Staff should use the recorded color, print, placement and scale when preparing production. The server cannot prove that a client-supplied preview matches the selection. This implementation does not claim photorealistic production dimensions or calibrated garment colors.

Payment proofs are readable only by staff with the `view_paymentproof` permission. Design previews and receipts require the purchasing browser session or `view_order` permission. Tracking requires both order ID and normalized phone and reveals status only, not the address or uploaded files.

See `docs/FILE_TREE.txt` for the complete file tree, and `docs/FULL_SOURCE.md` for all text source files organized by path. Font binaries are included in the ZIP. The implementation is split in logical order: settings, models/validation/services, views/URLs/admin, templates, CSS/JS, Docker/deployment, tests and documentation.

### config/settings.py


```python
import os
import secrets
from decimal import Decimal
from pathlib import Path
from django.core.exceptions import ImproperlyConfigured

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent


def flag(name, default="0"):
    return os.getenv(name, default).lower() in {"1", "true", "yes"}


DEMO_MODE = flag("DEMO_MODE", "1")
SECRET_KEY = os.environ.get("DJANGO_SECRET_KEY", "")
if not SECRET_KEY and DEMO_MODE:
    # Docker's entrypoint supplies one key to all workers. This fallback also lets
    # isolated demo management commands run without a checked-in secret.
    SECRET_KEY = os.environ["DJANGO_SECRET_KEY"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(64)
if not SECRET_KEY:
    raise ImproperlyConfigured("Set DJANGO_SECRET_KEY; Docker generates one for demo mode only.")
DEBUG = flag("DJANGO_DEBUG")
ALLOWED_HOSTS = os.getenv("DJANGO_ALLOWED_HOSTS", "localhost,127.0.0.1,[::1]").split(",")
CSRF_TRUSTED_ORIGINS = [x for x in os.getenv("CSRF_TRUSTED_ORIGINS", "").split(",") if x]
INSTALLED_APPS = [
    "django.contrib.admin", "django.contrib.auth", "django.contrib.contenttypes",
    "django.contrib.sessions", "django.contrib.messages", "django.contrib.staticfiles",
    "studio",
]
MIDDLEWARE = [
    "django.middleware.security.SecurityMiddleware", "whitenoise.middleware.WhiteNoiseMiddleware",
    "studio.middleware.RequestGuardMiddleware", "django.contrib.sessions.middleware.SessionMiddleware",
    "django.middleware.common.CommonMiddleware", "django.middleware.csrf.CsrfViewMiddleware",
    "django.contrib.auth.middleware.AuthenticationMiddleware", "django.contrib.messages.middleware.MessageMiddleware",
    "django.middleware.clickjacking.XFrameOptionsMiddleware",
]
ROOT_URLCONF = "config.urls"
WSGI_APPLICATION = "config.wsgi.application"
TEMPLATES = [{
    "BACKEND": "django.template.backends.django.DjangoTemplates",
    "DIRS": [BASE_DIR / "templates"], "APP_DIRS": True,
    "OPTIONS": {"context_processors": [
        "django.template.context_processors.request", "django.contrib.auth.context_processors.auth",
        "django.contrib.messages.context_processors.messages", "studio.context.brand",
    ]},
}]
# SQLite is an explicit local/test escape hatch. Docker always uses PostgreSQL.
if flag("USE_SQLITE"):
    DATABASES = {"default": {"ENGINE": "django.db.backends.sqlite3", "NAME": BASE_DIR / "local.sqlite3"}}
else:
    DATABASES = {"default": {
        "ENGINE": "django.db.backends.postgresql", "NAME": os.getenv("POSTGRES_DB", "babal"),
        "USER": os.getenv("POSTGRES_USER", "babal"), "PASSWORD": os.getenv("POSTGRES_PASSWORD", ""),
        "HOST": os.getenv("POSTGRES_HOST", "db"), "PORT": os.getenv("POSTGRES_PORT", "5432"),
        "CONN_MAX_AGE": 60, "CONN_HEALTH_CHECKS": True,
        "OPTIONS": {"connect_timeout": 5},
    }}
AUTH_PASSWORD_VALIDATORS = [
    {"NAME": "django.contrib.auth.password_validation.UserAttributeSimilarityValidator"},
    {"NAME": "django.contrib.auth.password_validation.MinimumLengthValidator"},
    {"NAME": "django.contrib.auth.password_validation.CommonPasswordValidator"},
    {"NAME": "django.contrib.auth.password_validation.NumericPasswordValidator"},
]
LANGUAGE_CODE = "en-us"
TIME_ZONE = "Asia/Kathmandu"
USE_I18N = True
USE_TZ = True
DEFAULT_AUTO_FIELD = "django.db.models.BigAutoField"
STATIC_URL = "/static/"
STATIC_ROOT = BASE_DIR / "staticfiles"
MEDIA_URL = "/media/"
MEDIA_ROOT = Path(os.getenv("MEDIA_ROOT", str(BASE_DIR / "media")))
PRIVATE_MEDIA_ROOT = Path(os.getenv("PRIVATE_MEDIA_ROOT", str(BASE_DIR / "private-media")))
STORAGES = {
    "default": {"BACKEND": "django.core.files.storage.FileSystemStorage"},
    "staticfiles": {"BACKEND": "whitenoise.storage.CompressedManifestStaticFilesStorage"},
    "private": {"BACKEND": "django.core.files.storage.FileSystemStorage", "OPTIONS": {
        "location": PRIVATE_MEDIA_ROOT, "base_url": "/_private/",
    }},
}
FILE_UPLOAD_MAX_MEMORY_SIZE = 1024 * 1024
DATA_UPLOAD_MAX_MEMORY_SIZE = 12 * 1024 * 1024
DATA_UPLOAD_MAX_NUMBER_FILES = 3
MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_IMAGE_PIXELS = 16_000_000
REQUEST_BODY_LIMIT = 12 * 1024 * 1024
TRUST_PROXY = flag("TRUST_PROXY")
SECURE_SSL_REDIRECT = flag("SECURE_SSL_REDIRECT")
SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE = flag("COOKIE_SECURE")
CSRF_COOKIE_SECURE = flag("COOKIE_SECURE")
SESSION_COOKIE_HTTPONLY = True
SESSION_COOKIE_SAMESITE = "Lax"
SECURE_CONTENT_TYPE_NOSNIFF = True
SECURE_REFERRER_POLICY = "same-origin"
X_FRAME_OPTIONS = "DENY"
SECURE_HSTS_SECONDS = int(os.getenv("SECURE_HSTS_SECONDS", "0"))
SECURE_HSTS_INCLUDE_SUBDOMAINS = flag("SECURE_HSTS_INCLUDE_SUBDOMAINS")
SECURE_HSTS_PRELOAD = flag("SECURE_HSTS_PRELOAD")
SECURE_REDIRECT_EXEMPT = [r"^health/$"]
if TRUST_PROXY:
    SECURE_PROXY_SSL_HEADER = ("HTTP_X_FORWARDED_PROTO", "https")
DEFAULT_TSHIRT_PRICE = Decimal(os.getenv("DEFAULT_TSHIRT_PRICE", "1200"))
DEFAULT_DELIVERY_FEE = Decimal(os.getenv("DEFAULT_DELIVERY_FEE", "150"))
LOGGING = {
    "version": 1, "disable_existing_loggers": False,
    "handlers": {"console": {"class": "logging.StreamHandler"}},
    "root": {"handlers": ["console"], "level": "INFO"},
}
```

### studio/models.py


```python
import secrets
import uuid
from decimal import Decimal
from django.conf import settings
from django.core.files.storage import storages
from django.core.validators import MinValueValidator, MaxValueValidator, RegexValidator, URLValidator
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from django.db import models
from django.utils import timezone
from .validators import validate_image, sanitized_image

SIZE_CHOICES = [(x, x) for x in ("S", "M", "L", "XL", "XXL")]
PLACEMENTS = [("chest", "Left chest"), ("center", "Front center"), ("back", "Back")]
PROVINCES = [(x, x) for x in ("Koshi", "Madhesh", "Bagmati", "Gandaki", "Lumbini", "Karnali", "Sudurpashchim")]
PAYMENTS = [("cod", "Cash on delivery"), ("esewa", "eSewa"), ("khalti", "Khalti"), ("fonepay", "FonePay")]


def private_storage():
    return storages["private"]


def order_number():
    return f"BW-{timezone.localdate():%y%m%d}-{secrets.token_hex(5).upper()}"


def instagram_url(value):
    from urllib.parse import urlparse
    URLValidator(schemes=["https"])(value)
    if urlparse(value).hostname not in {"instagram.com", "www.instagram.com"}:
        raise ValidationError("Use an https://www.instagram.com/ post link.")


class CleanImageModel(models.Model):
    image_fields = ()

    class Meta:
        abstract = True

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        for name in self.image_fields:
            field = getattr(self, name)
            if field and not field._committed:
                setattr(self, name, sanitized_image(field))
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)


class StoreSettings(models.Model):
    base_price = models.DecimalField(max_digits=9, decimal_places=2, default=settings.DEFAULT_TSHIRT_PRICE, validators=[MinValueValidator(0)])
    delivery_fee = models.DecimalField(max_digits=9, decimal_places=2, default=settings.DEFAULT_DELIVERY_FEE, validators=[MinValueValidator(0)])
    contact_phone = models.CharField(max_length=30, blank=True)
    delivery_note = models.CharField(max_length=250, default="Delivery availability and timing will be confirmed after your order.")
    size_note = models.TextField(default="Demo sizes S–XXL. Add your garment measurements and fit guidance in admin before launch.")

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        self.pk = 1
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    @classmethod
    def load(cls):
        return cls.objects.get_or_create(pk=1)[0]

    def __str__(self):
        return "Store pricing & delivery"


class TShirtColor(models.Model):
    name = models.CharField(max_length=50, unique=True)
    hex_code = models.CharField(max_length=7, validators=[RegexValidator(r"^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$", "Use a six-digit hex color.")])
    price_adjustment = models.DecimalField(max_digits=8, decimal_places=2, default=0, validators=[MinValueValidator(0)])
    sort_order = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(default=0)
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["sort_order", "id"]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.name


class Print(CleanImageModel):
    image_fields = ("image",)
    name = models.CharField(max_length=80, unique=True)
    image = models.ImageField(upload_to="prints/", validators=[validate_image], help_text="Transparent PNG recommended; JPEG/WebP accepted. Maximum 5 MB.")
    description = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True)
    surcharge = models.DecimalField(max_digits=8, decimal_places=2, default=0, validators=[MinValueValidator(0)])
    sort_order = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(default=0)
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    is_demo = models.BooleanField(default=False)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["sort_order", "id"]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.name


class Product(CleanImageModel):
    image_fields = ("image",)
    name = models.CharField(max_length=100, unique=True)
    category = models.CharField(max_length=40, default="Other goods")
    description = models.CharField(max_length=250, blank=True)
    image = models.ImageField(upload_to="products/", validators=[validate_image])
    price = models.DecimalField(max_digits=9, decimal_places=2, validators=[MinValueValidator(0)])
    instagram_post_url = models.URLField(blank=True, validators=[instagram_url])
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    is_demo = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    sort_order = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(default=0)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["sort_order", "id"]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.name


class PaymentOption(CleanImageModel):
    image_fields = ("qr_image",)
    provider = models.CharField(max_length=12, choices=PAYMENTS[1:], unique=True)
    account_name = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    account_number = models.CharField(max_length=80, blank=True)
    qr_image = models.ImageField(upload_to="payment-qr/", blank=True, validators=[validate_image])
    instructions = models.CharField(max_length=250, blank=True)
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=False)

    def clean(self):
        if self.is_active and not (self.qr_image or self.account_number):
            raise ValidationError("Add an actual payment QR or account number before enabling this method.")

    def __str__(self):
        return self.get_provider_display()


class Order(CleanImageModel):
    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        RECEIVED = "received", "Received"
        CONFIRMED = "confirmed", "Confirmed"
        PRINTING = "printing", "Printing"
        SHIPPED = "shipped", "Shipped"
        DELIVERED = "delivered", "Delivered"
        CANCELLED = "cancelled", "Cancelled"

    class PaymentStatus(models.TextChoices):
        UNPAID = "unpaid", "Pay on delivery"
        REVIEW = "review", "Proof awaiting review"
        PAID = "paid", "Verified paid"
        REJECTED = "rejected", "Proof rejected — contact us"

    image_fields = ("generated_image",)
    id = models.UUIDField(primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4, editable=False)
    number = models.CharField(max_length=25, default=order_number, unique=True, editable=False)
    submission_key = models.UUIDField(unique=True, editable=False)
    color = models.ForeignKey(TShirtColor, on_delete=models.PROTECT)
    print = models.ForeignKey(Print, on_delete=models.PROTECT)
    color_name = models.CharField(max_length=50)
    color_hex = models.CharField(max_length=7)
    print_name = models.CharField(max_length=80)
    placement = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=PLACEMENTS)
    print_scale = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(validators=[MinValueValidator(60), MaxValueValidator(140)])
    generated_image = models.ImageField(storage=private_storage, upload_to="designs/", validators=[validate_image])
    full_name = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    instagram_username = models.CharField(max_length=30, blank=True)
    phone = models.CharField(max_length=10)
    province = models.CharField(max_length=30, choices=PROVINCES)
    district = models.CharField(max_length=80)
    municipality = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    ward = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(validators=[MinValueValidator(1), MaxValueValidator(35)])
    landmark = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    size = models.CharField(max_length=4, choices=SIZE_CHOICES)
    quantity = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(validators=[MinValueValidator(1), MaxValueValidator(20)])
    notes = models.TextField(blank=True, max_length=1000)
    payment_method = models.CharField(max_length=12, choices=PAYMENTS)
    payment_status = models.CharField(max_length=12, choices=PaymentStatus, default=PaymentStatus.UNPAID)
    status = models.CharField(max_length=12, choices=Status, default=Status.RECEIVED)
    unit_price = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2)
    delivery_fee = models.DecimalField(max_digits=9, decimal_places=2)
    total = models.DecimalField(max_digits=11, decimal_places=2)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["-created_at"]
        constraints = [
            models.CheckConstraint(condition=models.Q(quantity__gte=1, quantity__lte=20), name="order_quantity_range"),
            models.CheckConstraint(condition=models.Q(total__gte=0), name="order_nonnegative_total"),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.number


class PaymentProof(CleanImageModel):
    image_fields = ("image",)
    order = models.OneToOneField(Order, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="payment_proof")
    image = models.ImageField(storage=private_storage, upload_to="proofs/", validators=[validate_image])
    submitted_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    reviewed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    reviewed_by = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, null=True, blank=True, on_delete=models.SET_NULL)
    admin_note = models.TextField(blank=True)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Payment proof / {self.order.number}"


class Review(CleanImageModel):
    image_fields = ("photo",)
    name = models.CharField(max_length=80)
    instagram_username = models.CharField(max_length=30, blank=True)
    rating = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(choices=[(n, str(n)) for n in range(1, 6)], validators=[MinValueValidator(1), MaxValueValidator(5)])
    text = models.TextField(max_length=1500)
    photo = models.ImageField(upload_to="reviews/", blank=True, validators=[validate_image])
    approved = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    is_demo = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["-created_at"]
        constraints = [models.CheckConstraint(condition=models.Q(rating__gte=1, rating__lte=5), name="review_rating_range")]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.name} / {self.rating} stars"


class RateLimitBucket(models.Model):
    key = models.CharField(max_length=64, primary_key=True)
    count = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)
    expires_at = models.DateTimeField(db_index=True)
```


### studio/validators.py


```python
import re
import warnings
from io import BytesIO
from uuid import uuid4
from PIL import Image, ImageOps, UnidentifiedImageError
from django.conf import settings
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from django.core.files.base import ContentFile


def normalize_phone(value):
    value = str(value).translate(str.maketrans("०१२३४५६७८९", "0123456789"))
    value = re.sub(r"[\s()-]", "", value)
    if value.startswith("+977"):
        value = value[4:]
    elif value.startswith("977") and len(value) == 13:
        value = value[3:]
    # Validate Nepal mobile syntax, not carrier ownership or reachability.
    if not re.fullmatch(r"9[678]\d{8}", value):
        raise ValidationError("Enter a 10-digit Nepali mobile number starting with 96, 97 or 98.")
    return value


def normalize_instagram(value):
    value = value.strip().lstrip("@")
    if value and not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9._]{1,30}", value):
        raise ValidationError("Use an Instagram username, without a link or spaces.")
    return value


def validate_image(upload):
    if upload.size > settings.MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES:
        raise ValidationError("Use a PNG, JPEG or WebP image smaller than 5 MB.")
    try:
        upload.seek(0)
        with warnings.catch_warnings():
            warnings.simplefilter("error", Image.DecompressionBombWarning)
            image = Image.open(upload)
            if image.format not in {"PNG", "JPEG", "WEBP"}:
                raise ValidationError("Only PNG, JPEG and WebP images are accepted. SVG and GIF are not supported.")
            if image.width * image.height > settings.MAX_IMAGE_PIXELS or max(image.size) > 6000:
                raise ValidationError("Image dimensions are too large. Maximum: 16 megapixels and 6000px per side.")
            image.verify()
    except (UnidentifiedImageError, OSError, ValueError, Image.DecompressionBombError, Image.DecompressionBombWarning):
        raise ValidationError("This image is damaged or is not a supported image.")
    finally:
        upload.seek(0)


def sanitized_image(upload, max_side=1800):
    """Decode and re-encode: never retain metadata or an untrusted original filename."""
    validate_image(upload)
    with Image.open(upload) as source:
        image = ImageOps.exif_transpose(source).convert("RGBA")
        image.thumbnail((max_side, max_side), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)
        output = BytesIO()
        image.save(output, format="PNG", optimize=True)
    upload.seek(0)
    return ContentFile(output.getvalue(), name=f"{uuid4().hex}.png")
```

```python
from django import forms
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from .models import Order, PaymentOption, Print, Review, TShirtColor
from .validators import normalize_phone, normalize_instagram, validate_image


class HoneyForm(forms.Form):
    website = forms.CharField(required=False, widget=forms.TextInput(attrs={"tabindex": "-1", "autocomplete": "off"}))

    def clean_website(self):
        if self.cleaned_data.get("website"):
            raise ValidationError("We couldn't submit this form. Please reload and try again.")
        return ""


class OrderForm(HoneyForm, forms.ModelForm):
    phone = forms.CharField(max_length=25, widget=forms.TextInput(attrs={"inputmode": "tel", "autocomplete": "tel", "placeholder": "98XXXXXXXX"}))
    submission_key = forms.UUIDField(widget=forms.HiddenInput)
    design_image = forms.ImageField(validators=[validate_image])
    payment_screenshot = forms.ImageField(required=False, validators=[validate_image])
    terms = forms.BooleanField(error_messages={"required": "Please confirm the design and delivery details."})

    class Meta:
        model = Order
        fields = ["color", "print", "placement", "print_scale", "full_name", "instagram_username", "phone", "province", "district", "municipality", "ward", "landmark", "size", "quantity", "notes", "payment_method"]
        widgets = {
            "notes": forms.Textarea(attrs={"rows": 3}),
            "phone": forms.TextInput(attrs={"inputmode": "tel", "autocomplete": "tel", "placeholder": "98XXXXXXXX"}),
            "full_name": forms.TextInput(attrs={"autocomplete": "name"}),
            "instagram_username": forms.TextInput(attrs={"placeholder": "@yourhandle", "autocapitalize": "none"}),
            "ward": forms.NumberInput(attrs={"min": 1, "max": 35}),
            "quantity": forms.NumberInput(attrs={"min": 1, "max": 20, "value": 1}),
        }

    def __init__(self, *args, checkout_keys=(), **kwargs):
        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
        self.checkout_keys = checkout_keys
        self.fields["color"].queryset = TShirtColor.objects.filter(is_active=True)
        self.fields["print"].queryset = Print.objects.filter(is_active=True)
        # Form-level length permits an optional country code; stored value stays 10 digits.
        self.fields["phone"].max_length = 25
        self.fields["phone"].widget.attrs["maxlength"] = 25
        for field in self.fields.values():
            field.widget.attrs.setdefault("class", "input")

    def clean_phone(self):
        return normalize_phone(self.cleaned_data["phone"])

    def clean_instagram_username(self):
        return normalize_instagram(self.cleaned_data.get("instagram_username", ""))

    def clean_submission_key(self):
        key = self.cleaned_data["submission_key"]
        if str(key) not in self.checkout_keys:
            raise ValidationError("Your checkout expired. Reload the designer to start a fresh order.")
        return key

    def clean(self):
        data = super().clean()
        method = data.get("payment_method")
        if method and method != "cod":
            if not PaymentOption.objects.filter(provider=method, is_active=True).exists():
                self.add_error("payment_method", "This payment method is not available yet. Choose cash on delivery.")
            if not data.get("payment_screenshot"):
                self.add_error("payment_screenshot", "Upload the payment screenshot for manual verification.")
        return data


class ReviewForm(HoneyForm, forms.ModelForm):
    consent = forms.BooleanField(label="You may publish my review, name, username and optional photo after moderation.")

    class Meta:
        model = Review
        fields = ["name", "instagram_username", "rating", "text", "photo"]
        widgets = {"text": forms.Textarea(attrs={"rows": 4, "placeholder": "How did you make it yours?"})}

    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
        for field in self.fields.values():
            field.widget.attrs.setdefault("class", "input")

    def clean_instagram_username(self):
        return normalize_instagram(self.cleaned_data.get("instagram_username", ""))


class TrackingForm(HoneyForm):
    number = forms.CharField(max_length=25, label="Order ID", widget=forms.TextInput(attrs={"placeholder": "BW-YYMMDD-XXXXXXXXXX", "autocapitalize": "characters"}))
    phone = forms.CharField(max_length=25, widget=forms.TextInput(attrs={"inputmode": "tel", "autocomplete": "tel"}))

    def clean_phone(self):
        return normalize_phone(self.cleaned_data["phone"])

    def clean_number(self):
        return self.cleaned_data["number"].strip().upper()
```


### studio/payments.py


```python
from dataclasses import dataclass
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class GatewaySession:
    reference: str
    redirect_url: str


class PaymentGateway(ABC):
    """Boundary for a future provider adapter; amounts must come from the saved order."""
    @abstractmethod
    def initiate(self, *, order_number: str, amount_paisa: int, return_url: str) -> GatewaySession:
        raise NotImplementedError("Implement the provider's authenticated initiation API.")

    @abstractmethod
    def verify(self, *, reference: str, amount_paisa: int) -> bool:
        raise NotImplementedError("Implement server-to-server amount, currency and reference verification.")


class GatewayNotConfigured(RuntimeError):
    pass


class DisabledGateway(PaymentGateway):
    """Intentionally disabled, never returns a fake payment success."""
    def initiate(self, *, order_number, amount_paisa, return_url):
        raise GatewayNotConfigured("Automatic payment gateways are not configured. Use QR proof or COD.")

    def verify(self, *, reference, amount_paisa):
        raise GatewayNotConfigured("Only an authenticated server-to-server adapter may verify a payment.")


gateway = DisabledGateway()
```


### studio/services.py


```python
from django.db import transaction
from .models import Order, PaymentProof, TShirtColor, Print, StoreSettings


def create_order(cleaned):
    """Prices and catalogue snapshots are fixed atomically on the server."""
    existing = Order.objects.filter(submission_key=cleaned["submission_key"]).first()
    if existing:
        return existing
    saved_files = []
    try:
        with transaction.atomic():
            color = TShirtColor.objects.select_for_update().get(pk=cleaned["color"].pk, is_active=True)
            artwork = Print.objects.select_for_update().get(pk=cleaned["print"].pk, is_active=True)
            store = StoreSettings.objects.select_for_update().get(pk=1)
            unit_price = store.base_price + color.price_adjustment + artwork.surcharge
            fields = ["submission_key", "placement", "print_scale", "full_name", "instagram_username", "phone", "province", "district", "municipality", "ward", "landmark", "size", "quantity", "notes", "payment_method"]
            order = Order(**{name: cleaned[name] for name in fields}, color=color, print=artwork,
                          color_name=color.name, color_hex=color.hex_code, print_name=artwork.name,
                          unit_price=unit_price, delivery_fee=store.delivery_fee,
                          total=unit_price * cleaned["quantity"] + store.delivery_fee,
                          payment_status=Order.PaymentStatus.UNPAID if cleaned["payment_method"] == "cod" else Order.PaymentStatus.REVIEW)
            order.generated_image = cleaned["design_image"]
            # Save file separately so a database rollback can remove it as well.
            from .validators import sanitized_image
            image = sanitized_image(order.generated_image)
            order.generated_image.save(image.name, image, save=False)
            saved_files.append(order.generated_image)
            order.save()
            if cleaned["payment_method"] != "cod":
                proof = PaymentProof(order=order)
                image = sanitized_image(cleaned["payment_screenshot"])
                proof.image.save(image.name, image, save=False)
                saved_files.append(proof.image)
                proof.save()
        return order
    except Exception:
        for file in saved_files:
            file.storage.delete(file.name)
        raise
```


### studio/middleware.py


```python
import hashlib
import time
from datetime import timedelta
from django.conf import settings
from django.db.models import F
from django.http import HttpResponse
from django.utils import timezone
from .models import RateLimitBucket


class RequestGuardMiddleware:
    """Database-backed limits work across Gunicorn workers without Redis."""
    limits = {"/orders/create/": (10, 600), "/reviews/submit/": (5, 3600), "/track/": (20, 600), "/admin/login/": (15, 600)}

    def __init__(self, get_response):
        self.get_response = get_response

    def __call__(self, request):
        if request.method == "POST":
            try:
                length = int(request.META.get("CONTENT_LENGTH") or 0)
            except ValueError:
                return HttpResponse("Invalid content length.", status=400)
            if length > settings.REQUEST_BODY_LIMIT:
                return HttpResponse("Upload too large. Each image must be under 5 MB.", status=413)
            if request.path in self.limits:
                limit, window = self.limits[request.path]
                ip = request.META.get("REMOTE_ADDR", "unknown")
                if settings.TRUST_PROXY:
                    ip = request.META.get("HTTP_X_REAL_IP", ip)
                key = hashlib.sha256(f"{request.path}:{ip}:{int(time.time()) // window}".encode()).hexdigest()
                bucket, _ = RateLimitBucket.objects.get_or_create(key=key, defaults={"expires_at": timezone.now() + timedelta(seconds=window)})
                RateLimitBucket.objects.filter(pk=key).update(count=F("count") + 1)
                bucket.refresh_from_db()
                if bucket.count > limit:
                    response = HttpResponse("Too many attempts. Please try again in a few minutes.", status=429)
                    response["Retry-After"] = str(window)
                    return response
        response = self.get_response(request)
        response["Permissions-Policy"] = "camera=(), microphone=(), geolocation=()"
        return response
```

### studio/views.py


```python
import uuid
from django.conf import settings
from django.contrib import messages
from django.contrib.admin.views.decorators import staff_member_required
from django.core.paginator import Paginator
from django.db import IntegrityError, connection
from django.db.models import Avg, Count
from django.http import FileResponse, Http404, JsonResponse
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404, redirect, render
from django.urls import reverse
from django.views.decorators.cache import never_cache
from django.views.decorators.http import require_GET, require_POST, require_http_methods
from .context import get_store
from .forms import OrderForm, ReviewForm, TrackingForm
from .models import Order, PaymentOption, PaymentProof, Print, Product, Review, TShirtColor
from .services import create_order


@require_GET
@never_cache
def designer(request):
    token = str(uuid.uuid4())
    request.session["checkout_keys"] = (request.session.get("checkout_keys", []) + [token])[-10:]
    colors = TShirtColor.objects.filter(is_active=True)
    prints = Print.objects.filter(is_active=True)
    store = get_store(request)
    catalogue = {
        "colors": [{"id": c.pk, "name": c.name, "hex": c.hex_code, "extra": str(c.price_adjustment)} for c in colors],
        "prints": [{"id": p.pk, "name": p.name, "url": p.image.url, "extra": str(p.surcharge), "description": p.description, "demo": p.is_demo} for p in prints],
        "basePrice": str(store.base_price), "delivery": str(store.delivery_fee),
    }
    return render(request, "studio/designer.html", {
        "catalogue": catalogue, "colors": colors, "prints": prints,
        "form": OrderForm(initial={"submission_key": token, "quantity": 1}, checkout_keys=[token]),
        "payment_options": PaymentOption.objects.filter(is_active=True),
    })


@require_POST
@never_cache
def order_create(request):
    keys = request.session.get("checkout_keys", [])
    key = request.POST.get("submission_key", "")
    # A retry after a lost response returns the original order, without a second charge/order.
    existing = Order.objects.filter(submission_key=key).first() if key in keys else None
    if existing:
        order = existing
    else:
        form = OrderForm(request.POST, request.FILES, checkout_keys=keys)
        if not form.is_valid():
            return JsonResponse({"errors": form.errors.get_json_data()}, status=422)
        try:
            order = create_order(form.cleaned_data)
        except (TShirtColor.DoesNotExist, Print.DoesNotExist):
            return JsonResponse({"errors": {"catalogue": [{"message": "This design is no longer available. Reload and choose another."}]}}, status=409)
        except IntegrityError:
            order = Order.objects.filter(submission_key=key).first()
            if order is None:
                raise
    request.session["order_ids"] = (request.session.get("order_ids", []) + [str(order.pk)])[-30:]
    return JsonResponse({"redirect": reverse("studio:done", args=[order.pk]), "order_number": order.number}, status=200 if existing else 201)


def authorized_order(request, pk):
    if not request.user.has_perm("studio.view_order") and str(pk) not in request.session.get("order_ids", []):
        raise Http404
    return get_object_or_404(Order, pk=pk)


@require_GET
@never_cache
def done(request, pk):
    return render(request, "studio/done.html", {"order": authorized_order(request, pk)})


@require_GET
@never_cache
def order_image(request, pk):
    order = authorized_order(request, pk)
    response = FileResponse(order.generated_image.open("rb"), content_type="image/png")
    response["Cache-Control"] = "private, no-store"
    response["Content-Disposition"] = f'inline; filename="{order.number}.png"'
    return response


@staff_member_required
@never_cache
def payment_proof(request, pk):
    if not request.user.has_perm("studio.view_paymentproof"):
        raise Http404
    proof = get_object_or_404(PaymentProof, pk=pk)
    response = FileResponse(proof.image.open("rb"), content_type="image/png")
    response["Cache-Control"] = "private, no-store"
    return response


@require_GET
def explore(request):
    products = Product.objects.filter(is_active=True)
    category = request.GET.get("category", "")
    if category:
        products = products.filter(category=category)
    context = {"products": Paginator(products, 12).get_page(request.GET.get("page")), "category": category,
               "categories": Product.objects.filter(is_active=True).order_by("category").values_list("category", flat=True).distinct()}
    return render(request, "studio/partials/products.html" if request.headers.get("HX-Request") else "studio/explore.html", context)


def review_context(form=None):
    approved = Review.objects.filter(approved=True, is_demo=False)
    return {"reviews": approved, "stats": approved.aggregate(average=Avg("rating"), count=Count("id")),
            "form": form or ReviewForm()}


@require_GET
def reviews(request):
    context = review_context()
    context["reviews"] = Paginator(context["reviews"], 24).get_page(request.GET.get("page"))
    return render(request, "studio/reviews.html", context)


@require_POST
def review_submit(request):
    form = ReviewForm(request.POST, request.FILES)
    success = form.is_valid()
    if success:
        review = form.save(commit=False)
        review.approved = False
        review.save()
        form = ReviewForm()
    if request.headers.get("HX-Request"):
        # A 200 fragment intentionally allows HTMX's default swap on validation errors.
        return render(request, "studio/partials/review_form.html", {"form": form, "submitted": success})
    if success:
        messages.success(request, "Thank you! Your review is waiting for moderation.")
        return redirect("studio:reviews")
    return render(request, "studio/reviews.html", review_context(form), status=400)


@require_http_methods(["GET", "POST"])
@never_cache
def track(request):
    form = TrackingForm(request.POST or None)
    order = None
    if request.method == "POST" and form.is_valid():
        order = Order.objects.filter(number=form.cleaned_data["number"], phone=form.cleaned_data["phone"]).first()
        if order is None:
            form.add_error(None, "No matching order. Check both your order ID and phone number.")
    # Status only: never return address, proof, or customer contact information.
    return render(request, "studio/track.html", {"form": form, "tracked_order": order})


@require_GET
def health(request):
    try:
        with connection.cursor() as cursor:
            cursor.execute("SELECT 1")
        return JsonResponse({"status": "ok"})
    except Exception:
        return JsonResponse({"status": "unavailable"}, status=503)
```

### studio/admin.py


```python
from django.contrib import admin, messages
from django.db import transaction
from django.db.models import Q
from django.urls import reverse
from django.utils import timezone
from django.utils.html import format_html
from .models import Order, PaymentOption, PaymentProof, Print, Product, Review, StoreSettings, TShirtColor


class ImageAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    readonly_fields = ("preview",)

    @admin.display(description="Preview")
    def preview(self, obj):
        field = getattr(obj, "image", None) or getattr(obj, "photo", None) or getattr(obj, "qr_image", None)
        if field:
            return format_html('<img src="{}" style="max-width:180px;max-height:180px;object-fit:contain" alt="Image preview">', field.url)
        return "No image"


@admin.register(TShirtColor)
class ColorAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ("name", "swatch", "price_adjustment", "sort_order", "is_active")
    list_editable = ("sort_order", "is_active")
    list_filter = ("is_active",)

    @admin.display(description="Color")
    def swatch(self, obj):
        return format_html('<span style="display:inline-block;width:28px;height:28px;border:1px solid #888;background:{}"></span> {}', obj.hex_code, obj.hex_code)


@admin.register(Print)
class PrintAdmin(ImageAdmin):
    list_display = ("name", "preview", "surcharge", "is_active", "is_demo", "sort_order")
    list_filter = ("is_active", "is_demo")
    list_editable = ("is_active", "sort_order")
    search_fields = ("name", "description")


@admin.register(Product)
class ProductAdmin(ImageAdmin):
    list_display = ("name", "category", "preview", "price", "is_active", "is_demo")
    list_filter = ("category", "is_active", "is_demo")
    search_fields = ("name",)


@admin.register(PaymentOption)
class PaymentOptionAdmin(ImageAdmin):
    list_display = ("provider", "account_name", "is_active")


@admin.register(StoreSettings)
class StoreSettingsAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    def has_add_permission(self, request):
        return not StoreSettings.objects.exists()

    def has_delete_permission(self, request, obj=None):
        return False


@admin.register(Review)
class ReviewAdmin(ImageAdmin):
    list_display = ("name", "instagram_username", "rating", "approved", "is_demo", "created_at")
    list_filter = ("approved", "rating", "is_demo")
    search_fields = ("name", "text", "instagram_username")
    actions = ("approve", "unpublish")

    @admin.action(description="Approve selected reviews")
    def approve(self, request, queryset):
        for review in queryset:
            review.approved = True
            review.save(update_fields=["approved"])
            self.log_change(request, review, "Approved review")

    @admin.action(description="Unpublish selected reviews")
    def unpublish(self, request, queryset):
        for review in queryset:
            review.approved = False
            review.save(update_fields=["approved"])
            self.log_change(request, review, "Unpublished review")


class ProofInline(admin.StackedInline):
    model = PaymentProof
    fields = ("proof_link", "submitted_at", "reviewed_at", "reviewed_by", "admin_note")
    readonly_fields = ("proof_link", "submitted_at", "reviewed_at", "reviewed_by")
    extra = 0
    can_delete = False

    def has_add_permission(self, request, obj=None):
        return False

    @admin.display(description="Private payment screenshot")
    def proof_link(self, obj):
        return format_html('<a href="{}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open payment proof</a>', reverse("studio:payment_proof", args=[obj.pk]))


@admin.register(Order)
class OrderAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ("number", "full_name", "phone", "size", "quantity", "total", "payment_method", "payment_status", "status", "created_at")
    list_filter = ("status", "payment_status", "payment_method", "size", "province", "created_at")
    search_fields = ("number", "full_name", "phone", "instagram_username")
    date_hierarchy = "created_at"
    inlines = (ProofInline,)
    exclude = ("generated_image",)
    readonly_fields = ("number", "submission_key", "design_preview", "created_at", "updated_at", "unit_price", "delivery_fee", "total", "payment_status", "color", "print", "color_name", "color_hex", "print_name", "print_scale", "placement", "size", "quantity", "payment_method")
    actions = ("mark_paid", "reject_proof", "mark_shipped")

    def has_add_permission(self, request):
        return False

    @admin.display(description="Design reference (placement and scale are recorded separately)")
    def design_preview(self, obj):
        return format_html('<a href="{0}"><img src="{0}" width="260" alt="Customer design"></a>', reverse("studio:order_image", args=[obj.pk]))

    @admin.action(description="Mark paid after manually checking payment")
    def mark_paid(self, request, queryset):
        with transaction.atomic():
            for order in queryset.select_for_update():
                if order.payment_method != "cod" and not PaymentProof.objects.filter(order=order).exists():
                    self.message_user(request, f"{order.number}: no proof to verify.", messages.WARNING)
                    continue
                order.payment_status = Order.PaymentStatus.PAID
                order.save(update_fields=["payment_status", "updated_at"])
                PaymentProof.objects.filter(order=order).update(reviewed_at=timezone.now(), reviewed_by=request.user)
                self.log_change(request, order, "Payment manually verified")

    @admin.action(description="Reject selected payment proofs")
    def reject_proof(self, request, queryset):
        for order in queryset.exclude(payment_method="cod"):
            order.payment_status = Order.PaymentStatus.REJECTED
            order.save(update_fields=["payment_status", "updated_at"])
            PaymentProof.objects.filter(order=order).update(reviewed_at=timezone.now(), reviewed_by=request.user)
            self.log_change(request, order, "Payment proof rejected")

    @admin.action(description="Mark eligible orders shipped")
    def mark_shipped(self, request, queryset):
        eligible = queryset.exclude(status__in=["cancelled", "delivered"]).filter(Q(payment_method="cod") | Q(payment_status="paid"))
        count = 0
        for order in eligible:
            order.status = Order.Status.SHIPPED
            order.save(update_fields=["status", "updated_at"])
            self.log_change(request, order, "Marked shipped")
            count += 1
        self.message_user(request, f"{count} order(s) marked shipped. Unverified prepaid orders were skipped.")
```

```python
from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand
from django.utils import timezone
from studio.models import RateLimitBucket


class Command(BaseCommand):
    help = "Remove expired rate-limit buckets. Run daily alongside clearsessions."

    def handle(self, *args, **options):
        count, _ = RateLimitBucket.objects.filter(expires_at__lt=timezone.now()).delete()
        self.stdout.write(f"Removed {count} expired rate-limit buckets.")
```


### studio/management/commands/seed_demo.py


```python
from io import BytesIO
from pathlib import Path
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont
from django.conf import settings
from django.core.files.base import ContentFile
from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand
from studio.models import StoreSettings, TShirtColor, Print, Product


def font(size):
    candidates = [Path("/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans-Bold.ttf"), Path("/usr/share/fonts/dejavu/DejaVuSans-Bold.ttf")]
    for path in candidates:
        if path.exists():
            return ImageFont.truetype(str(path), size)
    return ImageFont.load_default(size=size)


def png(image, name):
    buffer = BytesIO()
    image.save(buffer, "PNG", optimize=True)
    return ContentFile(buffer.getvalue(), name=name)


def print_art(index):
    image = Image.new("RGBA", (600, 600), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(image)
    if index == 0:
        d.rounded_rectangle((60, 40, 540, 560), 55, fill="#D5FF45")
        d.text((300, 92), "OFFLINE", font=font(67), anchor="mt", fill="#17191B")
        d.text((300, 455), "CLUB", font=font(70), anchor="mt", fill="#17191B")
        d.ellipse((175, 205, 425, 425), fill="#17191B")
        for x in [245, 350]:
            d.ellipse((x-12, 265, x+12, 310), fill="#D5FF45")
        d.arc((235, 290, 365, 370), 5, 175, fill="#D5FF45", width=9)
    elif index == 1:
        d.rounded_rectangle((55, 75, 545, 525), 45, fill="#C8BCF2")
        d.text((300, 120), "LOW", font=font(96), anchor="mt", fill="#17191B")
        d.text((300, 225), "BATTERY", font=font(58), anchor="mt", fill="#17191B")
        d.rounded_rectangle((140, 340, 440, 445), 15, outline="#17191B", width=12)
        d.rectangle((450, 370, 470, 415), fill="#17191B")
        d.rectangle((162, 362, 210, 425), fill="#FF785D")
    elif index == 2:
        d.ellipse((45, 45, 555, 555), fill="#375CFF")
        d.text((300, 175), "OWN", font=font(105), anchor="mt", fill="#F4F1E8")
        d.text((300, 300), "LANE", font=font(100), anchor="mt", fill="#F4F1E8")
        d.line((190, 435, 400, 435), fill="#D5FF45", width=15)
        d.polygon([(390, 405), (430, 435), (390, 465)], fill="#D5FF45")
    else:
        d.rounded_rectangle((45, 45, 555, 555), 40, fill="#FF785D")
        for x in range(100, 510, 60):
            d.line([(x, 175), (x-30, 260), (x+30, 345), (x, 425)], fill="#17191B", width=18)
        d.text((300, 465), "WAVEFORM", font=font(40), anchor="mt", fill="#17191B")
    return image


def product_art(index):
    colors = ["#DCE4D1", "#DED6F3", "#F1D4BD", "#D6DEED"]
    image = Image.new("RGB", (850, 1000), colors[index])
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(image)
    d.ellipse((160, 780, 700, 860), fill="#B5B6AD")
    if index in (0, 3):
        c = "#263F39" if index == 0 else "#4057A1"
        points = [(315, 230), (210, 265), (80, 620), (175, 680), (275, 470), (270, 805), (580, 805), (575, 470), (675, 680), (770, 620), (640, 265), (535, 230)]
        d.polygon(points, fill=c)
        d.ellipse((350, 210, 500, 280), fill=colors[index])
        d.rounded_rectangle((350, 580, 500, 700), 20, outline="#8B9F99", width=3)
        d.text((425, 420), "babal.", anchor="mm", font=font(44), fill="#EDEEDB")
    elif index == 1:
        d.pieslice((220, 300, 670, 770), 180, 360, fill="#25262C")
        d.polygon([(220, 535), (670, 535), (760, 655), (130, 655)], fill="#35363D")
        d.line((445, 320, 445, 530), fill="#75757F", width=4)
        d.text((445, 460), "b.", anchor="mm", font=font(70), fill="#D5FF45")
    else:
        d.rounded_rectangle((260, 210, 590, 500), 150, outline="#A49A7C", width=30)
        d.rectangle((190, 400, 660, 820), fill="#E7DFC9")
        d.text((425, 540), "TAKE", anchor="mm", font=font(60), fill="#F45C3F")
        d.text((425, 625), "IT EASY.", anchor="mm", font=font(55), fill="#F45C3F")
    return image


class Command(BaseCommand):
    help = "Seed clearly marked original demo assets. Safe to repeat; never overwrites catalogue edits or seeds fake reviews."

    def handle(self, *args, **options):
        StoreSettings.load()
        for i, (name, color) in enumerate([("Washed black", "#28292D"), ("Chalk", "#F4F0E5"), ("Moss", "#70826B"), ("Lilac", "#B5A4CC"), ("Burnt orange", "#CA6546")]):
            TShirtColor.objects.get_or_create(name=name, defaults={"hex_code": color, "sort_order": i})
        for i, name in enumerate(["Offline club", "Low battery", "Own lane", "Waveform"]):
            if not Print.objects.filter(name=f"{name} / demo").exists():
                Print.objects.create(name=f"{name} / demo", image=png(print_art(i), f"print-{i}.png"), description="Original demo artwork. Replace with an approved BABAL WEARS print.", is_demo=True, sort_order=i)
        for i, (name, category, price) in enumerate([("Off-duty crew", "Layers", 2200), ("Everyday cap", "Headwear", 650), ("Carry a little", "Bags", 850), ("After-hours hoodie", "Layers", 2600)]):
            if not Product.objects.filter(name=f"{name} / demo").exists():
                Product.objects.create(name=f"{name} / demo", category=category, price=price, image=png(product_art(i), f"product-{i}.png"), is_demo=True, sort_order=i, description="Illustrative product and price. Availability has not been confirmed by the brand.")
        self.stdout.write(self.style.SUCCESS("Demo catalogue ready. No customer reviews or payment accounts were invented."))
```


### studio/management/commands/wait_for_db.py


```python
import time
from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand, CommandError
from django.db import connections
from django.db.utils import OperationalError, InterfaceError


class Command(BaseCommand):
    help = "Wait for PostgreSQL to accept connections before migrations."

    def handle(self, *args, **options):
        for attempt in range(30):
            try:
                with connections["default"].cursor() as cursor:
                    cursor.execute("SELECT 1")
                self.stdout.write("Database ready.")
                return
            except (OperationalError, InterfaceError):
                connections["default"].close()
                self.stdout.write(f"Waiting for database ({attempt + 1}/30)")
                time.sleep(2)
        raise CommandError("Database unavailable after 30 attempts.")
```

```html
<!doctype html>

  <meta name="theme-color" content="#F4F1E8">

  <meta name="htmx-config" content='{"allowEval":false,"allowScriptTags":false,"selfRequestsOnly":true}'>
  <title>{% block title %}Make it yours · BABAL WEARS{% endblock %}</title>
  <link rel="icon" href="{% static 'studio/favicon.svg' %}" type="image/svg+xml">
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="{% static 'studio/site.css' %}">
  <script defer src="{% static 'studio/vendor/gsap.min.js' %}"></script>
  <script defer src="{% static 'studio/vendor/htmx.min.js' %}"></script>
  <script defer src="{% static 'studio/app.js' %}"></script>
  <script defer src="{% static 'studio/vendor/alpine.min.js' %}"></script>
</head>
<body>
  <a class="skip-link" href="#main">Skip to content</a>
  <div class="ambient" aria-hidden="true"><i></i><i></i></div>
  <header class="site-header">
    <a class="wordmark" href="{% url 'studio:designer' %}" aria-label="Babal Wears home">babal<span class="logo-star" aria-hidden="true">✳</span><small>WEARS / NEPAL</small></a>
    <nav class="primary-nav" aria-label="Main navigation">
      <a href="{% url 'studio:designer' %}" {% if request.resolver_match.url_name == 'designer' %}aria-current="page"{% endif %}>The studio<span>01</span></a>
      <a href="{% url 'studio:explore' %}" {% if request.resolver_match.url_name == 'explore' %}aria-current="page"{% endif %}>Explore<span>02</span></a>
      <a href="{% url 'studio:reviews' %}" {% if request.resolver_match.url_name == 'reviews' %}aria-current="page"{% endif %}>The people<span>03</span></a>
    </nav>
    <a class="header-track" href="{% url 'studio:track' %}">Track your tee <span aria-hidden="true">↗</span></a>
  </header>
  {% if demo_mode %}<div class="demo-strip">CONCEPT STUDIO <span>Demo artwork & prices. Replace with your approved brand catalogue.</span></div>{% endif %}
  {% for message in messages %}<div class="message glass" role="status">{{ message }}</div>{% endfor %}
  <main id="main">{% block content %}{% endblock %}</main>
  <footer class="site-footer"><span lang="ne">बबाल लगाऊ। <span lang="en">MAKE IT YOURS.</span></span><div><a href="{{ instagram_url }}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">@babalwears ↗</a><a href="{{ dm_url }}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Say hello ↗</a>{% if store.contact_phone %}<a href="tel:{{ store.contact_phone }}">{{ store.contact_phone }}</a>{% endif %}</div><span class="dhaka-thread" aria-hidden="true"></span><small>YOUR COLOR. YOUR PRINT. YOUR RULES.</small></footer>
  <div id="cursor" aria-hidden="true">DRAG</div>
  <div id="toast" class="toast glass" role="status" aria-live="polite"></div>
  {% block scripts %}{% endblock %}
</body>
</html>
```

```html
{% extends 'studio/base.html' %}
{% load static %}
{% block content %}
<div class="studio" id="studio">
  <nav class="stepper glass" aria-label="Design progress"><ol>
    <li><button type="button" data-goto="1" aria-current="step"><b>01</b><span>Color</span></button></li>
    <li><button type="button" data-goto="2" disabled><b>02</b><span>Print</span></button></li>
    <li><button type="button" data-goto="3" disabled><b>03</b><span>Reveal</span></button></li>
    <li><button type="button" data-goto="4" disabled><b>04</b><span>Details</span></button></li>
    <li><button type="button" disabled><b>05</b><span>Done</span></button></li>
  </ol><span class="stepper-note">A LITTLE YOU. A LOT BABAL.</span></nav>
  <noscript><p class="message">The live T-shirt designer needs JavaScript. You can still browse <a href="{% url 'studio:explore' %}">Explore</a> or <a href="{{ dm_url }}">order through Instagram DM</a>.</p></noscript>
  {% if not colors or not prints %}
  <div class="empty-state glass"><h1>The rail is getting ready.</h1><p>New colors and prints are coming. Say hello to arrange a custom order.</p><a class="button dark" href="{{ dm_url }}">Open Instagram DM ↗</a></div>
  {% else %}
  <section class="scene color-scene is-active" data-step="1" aria-labelledby="color-title">
    <div class="scene-copy">
      <p class="eyebrow"><span class="status-dot"></span> THE CUSTOM TEE STUDIO</p>
      <h1 class="display" id="color-title" tabindex="-1">MAKE IT<br><span class="outline">BABAL.</span></h1>
      <p class="intro-text">Good taste? Yours.<br>Blank canvas? Ours.</p>
      <div class="nepali-note"><span lang="ne">आफ्नै स्टाइल।</span><svg viewBox="0 0 120 45" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M3 9Q46 60 106 16M91 9L112 12L103 32"/></svg></div>
      <div class="color-selection"><span class="eyebrow">01 / PICK YOUR CANVAS</span><h2 id="color-name">{{ colors.0.name }}</h2>
        <div class="swatches" role="group" aria-label="T-shirt color">
          {% for color in colors %}<button type="button" class="swatch" style="--swatch:{{ color.hex_code }}" data-color="{{ forloop.counter0 }}" aria-label="{{ color.name }}" aria-pressed="{% if forloop.first %}true{% else %}false{% endif %}"><span></span></button>{% endfor %}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="wardrobe-panel">
      <div class="wardrobe-top"><span class="tiny-label">THE EVERYDAY. REMIXED.</span><span class="edition-tag">VOL. 01<br>CUSTOM CLUB</span></div>
      <div class="rail-line" aria-hidden="true"></div>
      <div class="wardrobe" id="wardrobe" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="T-shirt rail. Drag or swipe, or use left and right arrow keys.">
        <div class="rail-track" id="rail-track">
          {% for color in colors %}<button type="button" class="garment {% if forloop.first %}focused{% endif %}" data-shirt="{{ forloop.counter0 }}" aria-label="Choose {{ color.name }}" aria-pressed="{% if forloop.first %}true{% else %}false{% endif %}">
            {% include 'studio/partials/shirt.html' with color=color.hex_code key=forloop.counter %}
            <span class="garment-label"><b>{{ forloop.counter|stringformat:'02d' }}</b> {{ color.name }}</span>
          </button>{% endfor %}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="orbit-sticker" aria-hidden="true"><span>100%<br><strong>YOUR<br>ENERGY.</strong></span><i>✳</i></div>
      <div class="rail-bottom"><span>← DRAG TO FIND YOUR MOOD →</span><div><button type="button" class="icon-button" id="rail-prev" aria-label="Previous color">←</button><button type="button" class="icon-button" id="rail-next" aria-label="Next color">→</button></div></div>
    </div>
  </section>
  <section class="scene print-scene" data-step="2" hidden aria-labelledby="print-title">
    <div class="scene-copy"><p class="eyebrow">02 / WEAR YOUR PERSONALITY</p><h1 class="display medium" id="print-title" tabindex="-1">LESS BLANK.<br>MORE <em>YOU.</em></h1><p class="intro-text">A little loud. A little weird.<br>Exactly your kind of thing.</p>
      <div class="print-deck" role="group" aria-label="Choose your print">
        {% for print in prints %}<button type="button" class="print-card" data-print="{{ forloop.counter0 }}" aria-pressed="{% if forloop.first %}true{% else %}false{% endif %}" style="--tilt:{% cycle '-5deg' '3deg' '-2deg' '5deg' %}">
          <span class="print-index">{{ forloop.counter|stringformat:'02d' }} {% if print.is_demo %}<small>DEMO ART</small>{% endif %}</span><img src="{{ print.image.url }}" alt="{{ print.name }}" width="160" height="160" loading="lazy" decoding="async"><span class="print-name">{{ print.name }}</span>
        </button>{% endfor %}
      </div>
      <p class="fine-print">Prints are uploaded by the brand. Demo art is marked.</p>
    </div>
    <div class="composition-panel"><div class="preview-caption"><span class="tiny-label">LIVE PREVIEW</span><span id="view-label">FRONT VIEW</span></div><div class="shirt-stage"><span class="stage-ring" aria-hidden="true"></span><canvas id="print-canvas" data-preview width="1000" height="1120" role="img" aria-label="Live T-shirt design preview"></canvas><span class="small-sticker" aria-hidden="true">GOOD<br>CHOICE ↗</span></div>
      <div class="placement-controls glass"><fieldset><legend>Where does it go?</legend><div class="segmented"><button type="button" data-placement="chest" aria-pressed="false">Chest</button><button type="button" data-placement="center" aria-pressed="true">Center</button><button type="button" data-placement="back" aria-pressed="false">Back ↺</button></div></fieldset><label class="scale-control" for="print-scale"><span>Make some noise <output id="scale-value">100%</output></span><input type="range" id="print-scale" min="60" max="140" value="100" step="5"></label></div>
    </div>
  </section>
  <section class="scene reveal-scene" data-step="3" hidden aria-labelledby="reveal-title">
    <div class="scene-copy"><p class="eyebrow">03 / THE MAIN CHARACTER MOMENT</p><h1 class="display medium" id="reveal-title" tabindex="-1">WELL,<br>THAT'S<br><span class="outline">BABAL.</span></h1><p class="intro-text">One of one energy.<br>Made by you.</p><div class="reveal-tools"><button type="button" class="button glass" id="download-design">Save your design <span>↓</span></button><button type="button" class="button text-button" id="share-design">Send to your people ↗</button></div><p class="fine-print">Digital mockup. Garment color and print scale may vary slightly in production.</p></div>
    <div class="reveal-art"><div class="reveal-star" aria-hidden="true">✳</div><div class="reveal-canvas-wrap"><canvas id="reveal-canvas" data-preview width="1000" height="1120" role="img" aria-label="Your completed T-shirt design"></canvas></div><div class="design-ticket glass"><div><small>DESIGNED BY</small><strong>You. Obviously.</strong></div><span id="reveal-description"></span><i aria-hidden="true">↗</i></div></div>
  </section>
  <section class="scene details-scene" data-step="4" hidden aria-labelledby="details-title">
    <div class="details-heading"><p class="eyebrow">04 / LET'S MAKE IT REAL</p><h1 class="display medium" id="details-title" tabindex="-1">YOUR TEE.<br>YOUR DOOR.</h1></div>
    <form id="order-form" method="post" action="{% url 'studio:order_create' %}" enctype="multipart/form-data" class="checkout-form glass" x-data="checkout">
      {% csrf_token %}{{ form.submission_key }}
      <div class="honeypot" aria-hidden="true">{{ form.website.label_tag }}{{ form.website }}</div>
      <input type="hidden" name="color" id="order-color"><input type="hidden" name="print" id="order-print"><input type="hidden" name="placement" id="order-placement"><input type="hidden" name="print_scale" id="order-scale">
      <div id="order-errors" class="form-errors" role="alert" tabindex="-1" hidden></div>
      <fieldset><legend><span>01</span> The person behind the tee</legend><div class="form-grid">{% include 'studio/partials/field.html' with field=form.full_name %}{% include 'studio/partials/field.html' with field=form.instagram_username %}{% include 'studio/partials/field.html' with field=form.phone %}</div></fieldset>
      <fieldset><legend><span>02</span> Where we're headed</legend><div class="form-grid">{% include 'studio/partials/field.html' with field=form.province %}{% include 'studio/partials/field.html' with field=form.district %}{% include 'studio/partials/field.html' with field=form.municipality %}{% include 'studio/partials/field.html' with field=form.ward %}{% include 'studio/partials/field.html' with field=form.landmark extra_class='wide' %}</div><p class="fine-print">{{ store.delivery_note }}</p></fieldset>
      <fieldset><legend><span>03</span> The perfect fit</legend><div class="form-grid">{% include 'studio/partials/field.html' with field=form.size %}{% include 'studio/partials/field.html' with field=form.quantity %}{% include 'studio/partials/field.html' with field=form.notes extra_class='wide' %}</div><details class="size-note"><summary>Fit & size notes ↗</summary><p>{{ store.size_note }}</p></details></fieldset>
      <fieldset><legend><span>04</span> Your way to pay</legend>
        <div class="payment-tabs"><label><input type="radio" name="pay_timing" value="cod" x-model="timing" checked> Cash on delivery</label><label><input type="radio" name="pay_timing" value="first" x-model="timing" {% if not payment_options %}disabled{% endif %}> Pay first</label></div>
        <input type="hidden" name="payment_method" :value="timing === 'cod' ? 'cod' : provider" value="cod">
        <p x-show="timing === 'cod'" class="fine-print">Pay when your tee arrives. We'll confirm your order and delivery details.</p>
        {% if not payment_options %}<p class="fine-print">Pay First will be available when the brand adds its verified payment details.</p>{% endif %}
        <div x-show="timing === 'first'" x-cloak><div class="provider-options">{% for option in payment_options %}<label><input type="radio" name="provider_choice" value="{{ option.provider }}" x-model="provider"> {{ option.get_provider_display }}</label>{% endfor %}</div>
          {% for option in payment_options %}<div class="payment-info" x-show="provider === '{{ option.provider }}'">{% if option.qr_image %}<img src="{{ option.qr_image.url }}" width="180" height="180" alt="{{ option.get_provider_display }} payment QR" loading="lazy">{% endif %}<div><strong>{{ option.account_name }}</strong><p>{{ option.account_number }}</p><p>{{ option.instructions }}</p></div></div>{% endfor %}
          <label class="upload-field" for="id_payment_screenshot">Payment screenshot <small>PNG, JPEG or WebP · maximum 5 MB</small><input type="file" id="id_payment_screenshot" name="payment_screenshot" accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp" :required="timing === 'first'" :disabled="timing !== 'first'"></label><p class="fine-print">A screenshot is proof for review. Payment is confirmed only after manual verification.</p>
        </div>
      </fieldset>
      <label class="consent"><input type="checkbox" name="terms" required><span>I've checked my design and delivery details. My contact details may be used to fulfill this order.</span></label>
      <button type="submit" class="button dark full-width" id="submit-order">Make it official <span>↗</span></button>
    </form>
    <aside class="checkout-summary glass"><span class="eyebrow">YOUR GOOD IDEA</span><img id="checkout-preview" alt="Your selected T-shirt" width="500" height="600"><h2 id="summary-design"></h2><p id="summary-placement"></p><dl><div><dt>Tee + print</dt><dd id="summary-unit"></dd></div><div><dt>Quantity</dt><dd id="summary-quantity">1</dd></div><div><dt>Delivery</dt><dd id="summary-delivery"></dd></div><div class="total"><dt>Total</dt><dd id="summary-total"></dd></div></dl><p class="fine-print">All prices in Nepali rupees (NPR). Final pricing is calculated securely at checkout.</p><button type="button" class="text-button" data-goto="2">← Tweak your design</button></aside>
  </section>
  <div class="journey-dock glass" id="journey-dock"><button type="button" class="back-button" id="journey-back" aria-label="Back to previous step" hidden>← Back</button><div class="dock-caption"><span id="dock-step">YOUR FIRST GOOD DECISION</span><strong id="dock-selection">{{ colors.0.name }}</strong></div><div class="dock-price"><small>YOUR CUSTOM TEE</small><strong id="dock-price"></strong></div><button type="button" class="button dark" id="journey-next">That's my color <span>↗</span></button></div>
  {% endif %}
</div>
<div class="ticker" aria-hidden="true"><div>YOUR COLOR ✳ YOUR PRINT ✳ YOUR RULES ✳ आफ्नै स्टाइल ✳ YOUR COLOR ✳ YOUR PRINT ✳ YOUR RULES ✳ आफ्नै स्टाइल ✳</div></div>
{% endblock %}
{% block scripts %}{{ catalogue|json_script:'studio-data' }}{% endblock %}
```

```html
{% if products.has_other_pages %}<nav class="pagination" aria-label="Products pages">{% if products.has_previous %}<a href="?category={{ category|urlencode }}&page={{ products.previous_page_number }}">← Previous</a>{% endif %}<span>{{ products.number }} / {{ products.paginator.num_pages }}</span>{% if products.has_next %}<a href="?category={{ category|urlencode }}&page={{ products.next_page_number }}">Next →</a>{% endif %}</nav>{% endif %}
```

```html
{% extends 'studio/base.html' %}
{% block title %}The people wall · BABAL WEARS{% endblock %}
{% block content %}<div class="page-wrap"><header class="editorial-heading"><div><p class="eyebrow">03 / THE PEOPLE MAKE IT</p><h1 class="display medium">BIG FITS.<br><span class="outline">REAL WORDS.</span></h1></div><div class="review-score glass"><strong>{% if stats.average %}{{ stats.average|floatformat:1 }}{% else %}—{% endif %}<small>/ 5</small></strong><span>{{ stats.count }} approved review{{ stats.count|pluralize }}</span><a href="#review-form">Leave your mark ↘</a></div></header>
<div class="reviews-layout"><section class="review-wall" aria-label="Approved customer reviews">{% for review in reviews %}<article class="review-card glass"><div class="review-card-top"><span class="rating" aria-label="{{ review.rating }} out of 5 stars">{% for n in '12345' %}{% if forloop.counter <= review.rating %}★{% else %}☆{% endif %}{% endfor %}</span><span aria-hidden="true">↗</span></div>{% if review.photo %}<img src="{{ review.photo.url }}" alt="Photo shared by {{ review.name }}" width="500" height="500" loading="lazy" decoding="async">{% endif %}<blockquote>{{ review.text|linebreaksbr }}</blockquote><footer><strong>{{ review.name }}</strong>{% if review.instagram_username %}<span>@{{ review.instagram_username }}</span>{% endif %}</footer></article>{% empty %}<div class="first-review glass"><span class="giant-asterisk" aria-hidden="true">✳</span><h2>THIS WALL<br>HAS YOUR<br>NAME ON IT.</h2><p>Fresh wall. Real stories only.<br>Be the first to leave your mark.</p><a href="#review-form">Tell us how it felt ↘</a></div>{% endfor %}</section><aside>{% include 'studio/partials/review_form.html' %}</aside></div>
{% if reviews.has_other_pages %}<nav class="pagination" aria-label="Review pages">{% if reviews.has_previous %}<a href="?page={{ reviews.previous_page_number }}">← Previous</a>{% endif %}<span>{{ reviews.number }} / {{ reviews.paginator.num_pages }}</span>{% if reviews.has_next %}<a href="?page={{ reviews.next_page_number }}">Next →</a>{% endif %}</nav>{% endif %}</div>{% endblock %}
```
