### studio/static/studio/app.js


```javascript
(() => {
  'use strict';
  const $ = (selector, root = document) => root.querySelector(selector);
  const $$ = (selector, root = document) => [...root.querySelectorAll(selector)];
  const reduced = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)');
  const motion = (target, values) => {
    if (window.gsap && !reduced.matches) return gsap.to(target, values);
    if (window.gsap) return gsap.set(target, { ...values, duration: 0, delay: 0 });
    values.onComplete?.();
    return null;
  };
  let toastTimer;
  function toast(message) {
    const el = $('#toast');
    if (!el) return;
    el.textContent = message;
    el.classList.add('show');
    clearTimeout(toastTimer);
    toastTimer = setTimeout(() => el.classList.remove('show'), 4500);
  }

  // Alpine owns only payment-panel disclosure; the design/canvas engine is vanilla JS.
  document.addEventListener('alpine:init', () => {
    Alpine.data('checkout', () => ({ timing: 'cod', provider: '', init() {
      this.provider = this.$el.querySelector('[name="provider_choice"]')?.value || '';
    } }));
  });
  document.addEventListener('htmx:responseError', event => {
    toast(event.detail.xhr.status === 429 ? 'A few too many attempts. Try again in a few minutes.' : 'That request did not go through. Please try again.');
  });
  document.addEventListener('htmx:sendError', () => toast('Connection interrupted. Please try again.'));
  const cursor = $('#cursor');
  if (cursor && matchMedia('(pointer:fine)').matches) {
    document.addEventListener('pointermove', event => {
      cursor.style.left = `${event.clientX}px`;
      cursor.style.top = `${event.clientY}px`;
      cursor.classList.toggle('visible', !reduced.matches && Boolean(event.target.closest('#wardrobe')));
    });
    document.addEventListener('pointerleave', () => cursor.classList.remove('visible'));
  }

  const dataNode = $('#studio-data');
  if (!dataNode || !$('#rail-track')) return;
  const data = JSON.parse(dataNode.textContent);
  if (!data.colors.length || !data.prints.length) return;
  const state = { color: 0, print: 0, placement: 'center', scale: 100, step: 1, reached: 1 };
  try {
    const saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('babal-design-v1') || 'null');
    if (saved) {
      state.color = Math.max(0, data.colors.findIndex(c => c.id === saved.color));
      state.print = Math.max(0, data.prints.findIndex(p => p.id === saved.print));
      if (['center', 'chest', 'back'].includes(saved.placement)) state.placement = saved.placement;
      if (Number.isFinite(saved.scale)) state.scale = Math.round(Math.max(60, Math.min(140, saved.scale)) / 5) * 5;
    }
  } catch { /* Storage may be unavailable in private browsing; design still works. */ }
  const money = value => `NPR ${Number(value).toLocaleString('en-NP', { maximumFractionDigits: 2 })}`;
  const currentColor = () => data.colors[state.color];
  const currentPrint = () => data.prints[state.print];
  const unitPrice = () => Number(data.basePrice) + Number(currentColor().extra) + Number(currentPrint().extra);
  const mix = (hex, weight = .25) => {
    const values = hex.match(/[a-f\d]{2}/gi).map(x => parseInt(x, 16));
    const paper = [244, 241, 232];
    return `rgb(${values.map((v, i) => Math.round(v * weight + paper[i] * (1 - weight))).join(',')})`;
  };
  function persist() {
    try { localStorage.setItem('babal-design-v1', JSON.stringify({ color: currentColor().id, print: currentPrint().id, placement: state.placement, scale: state.scale })); } catch {}
  }
  function updatePrice() {
    const quantity = Math.max(1, Math.min(20, Number($('#id_quantity')?.value) || 1));
    $('#dock-price').textContent = money(unitPrice());
    $('#summary-unit').textContent = money(unitPrice());
    $('#summary-quantity').textContent = String(quantity);
    $('#summary-delivery').textContent = money(data.delivery);
    $('#summary-total').textContent = money(unitPrice() * quantity + Number(data.delivery));
  }
  function updateSelection() {
    $$('[data-color]').forEach(el => el.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(Number(el.dataset.color) === state.color)));
    $$('[data-shirt]').forEach(el => {
      const active = Number(el.dataset.shirt) === state.color;
      el.classList.toggle('focused', active);
      el.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(active));
    });
    $$('[data-print]').forEach(el => el.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(Number(el.dataset.print) === state.print)));
    $$('[data-placement]').forEach(el => el.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(el.dataset.placement === state.placement)));
    $('#color-name').textContent = currentColor().name;
    $('#dock-selection').textContent = state.step === 1 ? currentColor().name : `${currentColor().name} / ${currentPrint().name}`;
    $('#view-label').textContent = state.placement === 'back' ? 'BACK VIEW' : 'FRONT VIEW';
    $('#scale-value').textContent = `${state.scale}%`;
    $('#print-scale').value = state.scale;
    $('#reveal-description').textContent = `${currentColor().name} / ${currentPrint().name}`;
    $('#summary-design').textContent = `${currentColor().name} / ${currentPrint().name}`;
    $('#summary-placement').textContent = `${state.placement === 'chest' ? 'Left chest' : state.placement === 'back' ? 'Back' : 'Front center'} · ${state.scale}% scale`;
    $('#order-color').value = currentColor().id;
    $('#order-print').value = currentPrint().id;
    $('#order-placement').value = state.placement;
    $('#order-scale').value = state.scale;
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--ambient-color', mix(currentColor().hex, .38));
    updatePrice();
    persist();
    renderPreviews().catch(() => toast('This print could not load. Check your connection or choose another.'));
  }

  // Geometry is shared by the live canvas and exported PNG. Design metadata, not the
  // uploaded preview, is authoritative for production (client images are untrusted).
  const frontPath = 'M171 66L104 91L28 204L103 253L139 201L130 490Q250 515 370 490L361 201L397 253L472 204L396 91L329 66C305 87 288 96 250 96C212 96 195 87 171 66Z';
  const backPath = 'M171 66L104 91L28 204L103 253L139 201L130 490Q250 515 370 490L361 201L397 253L472 204L396 91L329 66C305 75 288 82 250 82C212 82 195 75 171 66Z';
  const loadedImages = new Map();
  function loadPrint(url) {
    if (loadedImages.has(url)) return loadedImages.get(url);
    const promise = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const image = new Image();
      image.decoding = 'async';
      image.onload = () => resolve(image);
      image.onerror = () => { loadedImages.delete(url); reject(new Error('Artwork unavailable')); };
      image.src = url;
    });
    loadedImages.set(url, promise);
    return promise;
  }
  function drawShirt(ctx, artwork) {
    const path = new Path2D(state.placement === 'back' ? backPath : frontPath);
    ctx.save();
    ctx.fillStyle = currentColor().hex;
    ctx.fill(path);
    ctx.save();
    ctx.clip(path);
    const light = ctx.createLinearGradient(80, 120, 430, 410);
    light.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,.21)');
    light.addColorStop(.33, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)');
    light.addColorStop(.68, 'rgba(0,0,0,.045)');
    light.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,.24)');
    ctx.fillStyle = light;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 500, 560);
    for (const [x, width] of [[137, 22], [336, 25]]) {
      const fold = ctx.createLinearGradient(x, 0, x + width, 0);
      fold.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
      fold.addColorStop(.45, 'rgba(0,0,0,.06)');
      fold.addColorStop(.65, 'rgba(255,255,255,.07)');
      fold.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
      ctx.fillStyle = fold;
      ctx.fillRect(x, 205, width, 285);
    }
    const side = (state.placement === 'chest' ? 66 : 160) * state.scale / 100;
    const fit = Math.min(side / artwork.naturalWidth, side / artwork.naturalHeight);
    const width = artwork.naturalWidth * fit;
    const height = artwork.naturalHeight * fit;
    const x = state.placement === 'chest' ? 307 : 250;
    const y = state.placement === 'chest' ? 180 : 270;
    ctx.drawImage(artwork, x - width / 2, y - height / 2, width, height);
    ctx.restore();
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,.12)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.1;
    ctx.stroke(path);
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,.16)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.stroke(new Path2D('M141 197L153 468M359 197L347 468M134 479Q250 501 366 479M36 195L109 241M391 241L464 195'));
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.16)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
    ctx.stroke(new Path2D('M136 482Q250 504 364 482M39 192L110 238M390 238L461 192'));
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,.22)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 8;
    ctx.stroke(new Path2D(state.placement === 'back' ? 'M177 66Q250 102 323 66' : 'M179 67Q250 132 321 67'));
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.17)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
    ctx.stroke(new Path2D(state.placement === 'back' ? 'M177 69Q250 106 323 69' : 'M179 72Q250 138 321 72'));
    if (state.placement !== 'back') {
      ctx.fillStyle = '#e6e2d7';
      ctx.fillRect(239, 98, 22, 12);
      ctx.font = 'bold 7px Inter, sans-serif';
      ctx.fillStyle = '#17191b';
      ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      ctx.fillText('b.', 250, 107);
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }
  let renderGeneration = 0;
  async function renderPreviews() {
    const generation = ++renderGeneration;
    const artwork = await loadPrint(currentPrint().url);
    if (generation !== renderGeneration) return;
    $$('canvas[data-preview]').forEach(canvas => {
      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      ctx.save();
      ctx.scale(canvas.width / 500, canvas.height / 560);
      drawShirt(ctx, artwork);
      ctx.restore();
      canvas.setAttribute('aria-label', `${currentColor().name} T-shirt with ${currentPrint().name}, ${state.placement}, ${state.scale}% size`);
    });
  }
  async function exportCanvas() {
    const artwork = await loadPrint(currentPrint().url);
    if (document.fonts?.ready) await document.fonts.ready;
    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    canvas.width = 1000;
    canvas.height = 1200;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    const gradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 1000, 1200);
    gradient.addColorStop(0, '#f4f1e8');
    gradient.addColorStop(1, mix(currentColor().hex, .25));
    ctx.fillStyle = gradient;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 1000, 1200);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#d5ff45';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(700, 500, 270, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = '#17191b';
    ctx.font = '900 35px Inter, sans-serif';
    ctx.fillText('babal / MADE BY YOU', 50, 65);
    ctx.font = '500 16px Inter, sans-serif';
    ctx.fillText(`DESIGN PREVIEW · ${state.placement === 'back' ? 'BACK' : 'FRONT'} VIEW`, 50, 97);
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(50, 95);
    ctx.scale(1.8, 1.8);
    ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,.12)';
    ctx.shadowBlur = 12;
    ctx.shadowOffsetY = 12;
    const silhouette = new Path2D(state.placement === 'back' ? backPath : frontPath);
    ctx.fillStyle = currentColor().hex;
    ctx.fill(silhouette);
    ctx.shadowColor = 'transparent';
    drawShirt(ctx, artwork);
    ctx.restore();
    ctx.fillStyle = '#17191b';
    ctx.font = '700 25px Inter, sans-serif';
    ctx.fillText(`${currentColor().name} / ${currentPrint().name}`, 50, 1100, 900);
    ctx.font = '400 18px Inter, sans-serif';
    ctx.fillText(`${state.placement.toUpperCase()} · ${state.scale}% PRINT SCALE · @babalwears`, 50, 1135);
    ctx.font = '400 13px Inter, sans-serif';
    ctx.fillText('Digital mockup. Confirm garment color and print dimensions before production.', 50, 1170);
    return canvas;
  }
  async function designBlob() {
    const canvas = await exportCanvas();
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => canvas.toBlob(blob => blob ? resolve(blob) : reject(new Error('Could not create your PNG.')), 'image/png'));
  }
  function download(blob) {
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = url;
    link.download = 'my-babal-tee.png';
    document.body.append(link);
    link.click();
    link.remove();
    setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 30000);
  }
  $('#download-design').addEventListener('click', async event => {
    const button = event.currentTarget;
    button.disabled = true;
    try { download(await designBlob()); toast('Saved. Good taste looks good on you.'); }
    catch { toast('Your design could not be saved. Please try again.'); }
    finally { button.disabled = false; }
  });
  $('#share-design').addEventListener('click', async () => {
    try {
      const blob = await designBlob();
      const file = new File([blob], 'my-babal-tee.png', { type: 'image/png' });
      if (navigator.share && navigator.canShare?.({ files: [file] })) await navigator.share({ files: [file], title: 'I made it babal.', text: 'My custom BABAL WEARS tee.' });
      else { download(blob); toast('PNG saved — share it with your people.'); }
    } catch (error) { if (error.name !== 'AbortError') toast('Sharing is unavailable. Use Save your design to download the PNG.'); }
  });

  const rail = $('#wardrobe');
  const track = $('#rail-track');
  const garments = $$('[data-shirt]');
  const position = { x: 0 };
  let drag = null;
  let ignoreClickUntil = 0;
  let wheelTimer;
  const targetFor = index => rail.clientWidth * .40 - (garments[index].offsetLeft + garments[index].offsetWidth / 2);
  const clampX = value => Math.max(targetFor(garments.length - 1), Math.min(targetFor(0), value));
  function applyPosition() {
    track.style.transform = `translate3d(${position.x}px,0,0)`;
  }
  function nearest() {
    let index = 0;
    let distance = Infinity;
    garments.forEach((el, i) => {
      const delta = Math.abs(position.x - targetFor(i));
      if (delta < distance) { distance = delta; index = i; }
    });
    return index;
  }
  function focusColor(index, animate = true) {
    state.color = (index + data.colors.length) % data.colors.length;
    updateSelection();
    if (window.gsap) gsap.killTweensOf(position);
    const target = targetFor(state.color);
    if (animate && !reduced.matches && window.gsap) {
      gsap.to(position, { x: target, duration: .65, ease: 'power3.out', onUpdate: applyPosition });
      sway(2);
    } else { position.x = target; applyPosition(); }
  }
  function sway(velocity) {
    if (!window.gsap || reduced.matches) return;
    const shirts = $$('.shirt-svg', track);
    gsap.killTweensOf(shirts);
    gsap.to(shirts, { rotation: Math.max(-8, Math.min(8, velocity * 3)), duration: .12, overwrite: true,
      onComplete: () => gsap.to(shirts, { rotation: 0, duration: 1.15, ease: 'elastic.out(1,.35)', stagger: .025 }) });
  }
  rail.addEventListener('pointerdown', event => {
    if (event.button !== 0 && event.pointerType === 'mouse') return;
    if (window.gsap) gsap.killTweensOf(position);
    drag = { id: event.pointerId, startX: event.clientX, startY: event.clientY, base: position.x, lastX: event.clientX, time: performance.now(), velocity: 0, started: false };
  });
  rail.addEventListener('pointermove', event => {
    if (!drag || drag.id !== event.pointerId) return;
    const dx = event.clientX - drag.startX;
    const dy = event.clientY - drag.startY;
    if (!drag.started) {
      if (Math.abs(dy) > Math.abs(dx) && Math.abs(dy) > 10) { drag = null; return; }
      if (Math.abs(dx) < 6) return;
      drag.started = true;
      rail.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);
      rail.classList.add('dragging');
    }
    const now = performance.now();
    const elapsed = Math.max(1, now - drag.time);
    drag.velocity = .65 * drag.velocity + .35 * ((event.clientX - drag.lastX) / elapsed);
    drag.lastX = event.clientX;
    drag.time = now;
    position.x = clampX(drag.base + dx);
    applyPosition();
    if (nearest() !== state.color) { state.color = nearest(); updateSelection(); }
    if (window.gsap && !reduced.matches) gsap.to($$('.shirt-svg', track), { rotation: Math.max(-8, Math.min(8, drag.velocity * 4)), duration: .18, overwrite: true });
  });
  function release(event) {
    if (!drag || drag.id !== event.pointerId) return;
    const prior = drag;
    drag = null;
    rail.classList.remove('dragging');
    if (rail.hasPointerCapture(event.pointerId)) rail.releasePointerCapture(event.pointerId);
    if (!prior.started) return;
    ignoreClickUntil = performance.now() + 350;
    const velocity = performance.now() - prior.time > 120 ? 0 : prior.velocity;
    const target = clampX(position.x + velocity * 210);
    sway(velocity);
    if (window.gsap && !reduced.matches) gsap.to(position, { x: target, duration: .65, ease: 'power3.out', onUpdate() {
      applyPosition();
      if (nearest() !== state.color) { state.color = nearest(); updateSelection(); }
    }, onComplete: () => focusColor(nearest()) });
    else focusColor(nearest(), false);
  }
  rail.addEventListener('pointerup', release);
  rail.addEventListener('pointercancel', release);
  rail.addEventListener('wheel', event => {
    if (event.ctrlKey) return;
    const delta = Math.abs(event.deltaX) > Math.abs(event.deltaY) ? event.deltaX : event.deltaY;
    const next = clampX(position.x - delta);
    // At either end, hand vertical scrolling back to the page.
    if (Math.abs(next - position.x) < .5) return;
    event.preventDefault();
    if (window.gsap) gsap.killTweensOf(position);
    position.x = next;
    applyPosition();
    const index = nearest();
    if (index !== state.color) { state.color = index; updateSelection(); }
    sway(-delta / 30);
    clearTimeout(wheelTimer);
    wheelTimer = setTimeout(() => focusColor(nearest()), 170);
  }, { passive: false });
  rail.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
    if (event.key === 'ArrowRight' || event.key === 'ArrowLeft') { event.preventDefault(); focusColor(state.color + (event.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 1 : -1)); }
    if (event.key === 'Home') { event.preventDefault(); focusColor(0); }
    if (event.key === 'End') { event.preventDefault(); focusColor(garments.length - 1); }
  });
  $$('[data-color]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('click', () => focusColor(Number(el.dataset.color))));
  garments.forEach(el => el.addEventListener('click', () => { if (performance.now() > ignoreClickUntil) focusColor(Number(el.dataset.shirt)); }));
  $('#rail-prev').addEventListener('click', () => focusColor(state.color - 1));
  $('#rail-next').addEventListener('click', () => focusColor(state.color + 1));
  const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(() => { if (state.step === 1) focusColor(state.color, false); });
  resizeObserver.observe(rail);
  $$('[data-print]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('click', () => { state.print = Number(el.dataset.print); updateSelection(); }));
  $$('[data-placement]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('click', () => { state.placement = el.dataset.placement; updateSelection(); }));
  $('#print-scale').addEventListener('input', event => { state.scale = Number(event.target.value); updateSelection(); });
  $('#id_quantity').addEventListener('input', updatePrice);

  let transitioning = false;
  const nextLabels = ["", "That's my color", "Reveal my tee", "Let's make it real"];
  const stepLabels = ['', 'YOUR FIRST GOOD DECISION', 'A LITTLE PERSONALITY GOES A LONG WAY', 'LOOKING VERY YOU'];
  async function goTo(step) {
    if (transitioning || step < 1 || step > 4 || step > state.reached + 1) return;
    transitioning = true;
    let clone = null;
    try {
      await renderPreviews();
      if (state.step === 1 && step === 2 && window.gsap && !reduced.matches) {
        const source = $('.shirt-svg', garments[state.color]);
        const rect = source.getBoundingClientRect();
        clone = source.cloneNode(true);
        $('.hanger', clone)?.remove();
        Object.assign(clone.style, { position: 'fixed', left: `${rect.left}px`, top: `${rect.top}px`, width: `${rect.width}px`, height: `${rect.height}px`, maxWidth: 'none', zIndex: '40', pointerEvents: 'none', filter: 'drop-shadow(12px 20px 14px #0002)' });
        document.body.append(clone);
      }
      state.step = step;
      state.reached = Math.max(state.reached, step);
      $$('.scene').forEach(scene => { scene.hidden = Number(scene.dataset.step) !== step; });
      $$('.stepper [data-goto]').forEach(button => {
        const n = Number(button.dataset.goto);
        button.disabled = n > state.reached;
        if (n === step) button.setAttribute('aria-current', 'step'); else button.removeAttribute('aria-current');
      });
      $('#journey-back').hidden = step === 1;
      $('#journey-dock').hidden = step === 4;
      if (step < 4) { $('#journey-next').firstChild.textContent = `${nextLabels[step]} `; $('#dock-step').textContent = stepLabels[step]; }
      updateSelection();
      const scene = $(`[data-step="${step}"]`);
      const heading = $('h1', scene);
      heading.focus({ preventScroll: true });
      const studioTop = $('#studio').getBoundingClientRect().top + window.scrollY;
      if (window.scrollY > studioTop + 80) window.scrollTo({ top: studioTop, behavior: 'instant' });
      if (step === 1) focusColor(state.color, false);
      if (clone) {
        const canvas = $('#print-canvas');
        const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
        canvas.style.opacity = '0';
        await new Promise(resolve => gsap.timeline({ onComplete: resolve })
          .to(clone, { rotation: -8, y: -20, duration: .16 })
          .to(clone, { left: rect.left, top: rect.top, width: rect.width, height: rect.height, rotation: 0, y: 0, duration: .55, ease: 'power3.inOut' })
          .to(clone, { opacity: 0, duration: .18 })
          .to(canvas, { opacity: 1, duration: .18 }, '<'));
        clone.remove();
        clone = null;
      } else if (step !== 3) motion(scene, { opacity: 1, y: 0, startAt: { opacity: .3, y: 12 }, duration: .45, ease: 'power2.out' });
      if (step === 3) {
        motion($('.reveal-canvas-wrap'), { rotationY: 360, scale: 1, rotation: 0, startAt: { rotationY: 130, scale: .82, rotation: -5 }, duration: 1.1, ease: 'power3.out' });
        motion($('.design-ticket'), { y: 0, opacity: 1, startAt: { y: 25, opacity: 0 }, delay: .55, duration: .4 });
      }
      if (step === 4) $('#checkout-preview').src = (await exportCanvas()).toDataURL('image/png');
    } catch { toast('The preview is still loading. Please try again.'); }
    finally { clone?.remove(); $('#print-canvas').style.opacity = '1'; transitioning = false; }
  }
  $('#journey-next').addEventListener('click', () => goTo(state.step + 1));
  $('#journey-back').addEventListener('click', () => goTo(state.step - 1));
  $$('[data-goto]').forEach(button => button.addEventListener('click', () => goTo(Number(button.dataset.goto))));

  const form = $('#order-form');
  const errorsBox = $('#order-errors');
  const submit = $('#submit-order');
  const phone = $('#id_phone');
  phone.addEventListener('input', () => phone.setCustomValidity(''));
  phone.addEventListener('blur', () => {
    let value = phone.value.replace(/[०-९]/g, digit => '०१२३४५६७८९'.indexOf(digit)).replace(/[\s()-]/g, '').replace(/^(\+977|977(?=\d{10}$))/, '');
    phone.setCustomValidity(value && !/^9[678]\d{8}$/.test(value) ? 'Enter a 10-digit Nepali mobile number starting with 96, 97 or 98.' : '');
  });
  form.addEventListener('submit', async event => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (submit.disabled) return;
    errorsBox.hidden = true;
    $$('[aria-invalid]', form).forEach(el => el.removeAttribute('aria-invalid'));
    const upload = $('#id_payment_screenshot');
    if (!upload.disabled && upload.files[0]?.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) { toast('Payment screenshot must be smaller than 5 MB.'); upload.focus(); return; }
    submit.disabled = true;
    submit.textContent = 'Making it official…';
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 45000);
    try {
      const body = new FormData(form);
      body.set('design_image', await designBlob(), 'my-babal-tee.png');
      const response = await fetch(form.action, { method: 'POST', body, credentials: 'same-origin', headers: { Accept: 'application/json' }, signal: controller.signal });
      const contentType = response.headers.get('content-type') || '';
      const result = contentType.includes('application/json') ? await response.json() : null;
      if (response.ok && result?.redirect) { window.location.assign(result.redirect); return; }
      const errors = result?.errors || { request: [{ message: response.status === 429 ? 'Too many attempts. Please wait a few minutes.' : response.status === 403 ? 'Your session expired. Reload the page before submitting again.' : response.status === 413 ? 'The upload is too large. Use smaller images.' : 'Something went wrong. Please try again; duplicate submissions will not create another order.' }] };
      errorsBox.replaceChildren();
      const title = document.createElement('strong');
      title.textContent = 'A few details need another look:';
      const list = document.createElement('ul');
      for (const [field, messages] of Object.entries(errors)) {
        const input = form.elements.namedItem(field);
        if (input?.setAttribute) input.setAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true');
        messages.forEach(error => { const item = document.createElement('li'); item.textContent = error.message; list.append(item); });
      }
      errorsBox.append(title, list);
      errorsBox.hidden = false;
      errorsBox.focus();
    } catch {
      errorsBox.textContent = 'Connection interrupted. Check your connection and submit again. If the order reached us, the same order will be returned.';
      errorsBox.hidden = false;
      errorsBox.focus();
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timeout);
      submit.disabled = false;
      submit.textContent = 'Make it official ↗';
    }
  });
  focusColor(state.color, false);
})();
```

### studio/static/studio/site.css


```css
@font-face {
  font-family:Inter;
  src:url('vendor/inter-latin.woff2') format('woff2');
  font-weight:100 900;
  font-style:normal;
  font-display:swap
}

@font-face {
  font-family:Noto;
  src:url('vendor/noto-devanagari.woff2') format('woff2');
  font-weight:100 900;
  font-style:normal;
  font-display:swap
}

:root {
  --ink:#17191b;
  --paper:#f4f1e8;
  --lime:#d5ff45;
  --cobalt:#375cff;
  --coral:#ff785d;
  --lilac:#c8bcf2;
  --muted:#555952;
  --line:rgba(23,25,27,.17);
  --glass-bg:rgba(255,255,249,.72);
  --glass-border:rgba(255,255,255,.85);
  --glass-shadow:0 12px 40px rgba(28,32,25,.07);
  --glass-blur:20px;
  --radius:18px;
  --page-gutter:clamp(20px,4vw,72px);
  --ambient-color:#dce1ce;
  --font:Inter,Noto,Arial,sans-serif;
  --space:8px;
}

*,*::before,*::after {
  box-sizing:border-box
}

html {
  scroll-behavior:smooth;
  scroll-padding-top:110px;
  overflow-x:clip
}

body {
  margin:0;
  background:var(--paper);
  color:var(--ink);
  font-family:var(--font);
  font-size:15px;
  line-height:1.55;
  overflow-x:hidden
}

button,input,textarea,select {
  font:inherit;
  color:inherit
}

button,a,input,select,textarea {
  -webkit-tap-highlight-color:transparent
}

button {
  cursor:pointer
}

button:disabled {
  cursor:not-allowed
}

a {
  color:inherit;
  text-decoration:none
}

a:hover {
  text-decoration:none
}

button {
  border:0;
  background:none
}

img,svg {
  max-width:100%;
  display:block
}

img {
  height:auto
}

h1,h2,h3,p {
  margin:0
}

h1,h2,h3 {
  line-height:1.05;
  letter-spacing:-.045em
}

p+p {
  margin-top:12px
}

fieldset {
  border:0;
  padding:0;
  margin:0;
  min-width:0
}

legend {
  padding:0
}

ul {
  padding-left:22px
}

input[type=radio],input[type=checkbox] {
  accent-color:var(--ink);
  width:18px;
  height:18px;
  flex:none
}

::selection {
  background:var(--lime);
  color:var(--ink)
}

:focus-visible {
  outline:3px solid var(--cobalt);
  outline-offset:5px
}

[hidden],[x-cloak] {
  display:none!important
}

.skip-link {
  position:fixed;
  top:-80px;
  left:20px;
  z-index:100;
  background:var(--ink);
  color:white;
  padding:14px
}

.skip-link:focus {
  top:12px
}

.ambient {
  position:fixed;
  inset:0;
  pointer-events:none;
  z-index:-1;
  overflow:hidden
}

.ambient i {
  position:absolute;
  display:block;
  border-radius:50%;
  filter:blur(70px);
  opacity:.6;
  transition:background 1s ease
}

.ambient i:first-child {
  width:75vw;
  height:75vw;
  right:-20vw;
  top:-5vw;
  background:var(--ambient-color)
}

.ambient i:last-child {
  width:40vw;
  height:40vw;
  left:-25vw;
  bottom:-20vw;
  background:#e9e3d7
}

.glass {
  background:var(--glass-bg);
  border:1px solid var(--glass-border);
  box-shadow:var(--glass-shadow);
  backdrop-filter:blur(var(--glass-blur));
  -webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(var(--glass-blur));
  border-radius:var(--radius)
}

.site-header {
  min-height:96px;
  margin:0 var(--page-gutter);
  display:flex;
  align-items:center;
  justify-content:space-between;
  gap:24px;
  border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);
  position:relative;
  z-index:12
}

.wordmark {
  font-size:43px;
  font-weight:900;
  letter-spacing:-3px;
  line-height:.9;
  position:relative;
  display:block;
  padding-right:24px;
  transform:rotate(-3deg)
}

.wordmark small {
  display:block;
  font-size:8px;
  line-height:1;
  letter-spacing:2.4px;
  margin-top:9px;
  transform:rotate(3deg);
  padding-left:2px
}

.logo-star {
  position:absolute;
  font-size:29px;
  right:0;
  top:-3px;
  color:var(--ink)
}

.primary-nav {
  display:flex;
  gap:36px;
  align-items:center
}

.primary-nav a {
  font-size:12px;
  font-weight:650;
  display:flex;
  gap:6px;
  align-items:flex-start;
  padding:13px 0;
  position:relative
}

.primary-nav a span {
  font-size:8px;
  line-height:1.2
}

.primary-nav a::after {
  content:'';
  position:absolute;
  bottom:3px;
  left:0;
  right:0;
  height:2px;
  background:var(--ink);
  transform:scaleX(0);
  transform-origin:left;
  transition:transform .25s
}

.primary-nav a:hover::after,.primary-nav a[aria-current]::after {
  transform:scaleX(1)
}

.header-track {
  font-size:11px;
  display:flex;
  gap:30px;
  align-items:center;
  font-weight:600
}

.header-track span {
  font-size:22px
}

.demo-strip {
  margin:12px var(--page-gutter) 0;
  font-size:9px;
  letter-spacing:1px;
  font-weight:750;
  display:flex;
  gap:14px;
  color:#4c5149
}

.demo-strip span {
  font-weight:450;
  letter-spacing:.2px
}

.studio,.page-wrap {
  max-width:1800px;
  margin:0 auto;
  padding:24px var(--page-gutter) 0
}

.stepper {
  padding:7px 12px;
  display:flex;
  justify-content:space-between;
  align-items:center;
  position:sticky;
  top:12px;
  z-index:15;
  border-radius:50px;
  min-height:56px
}

.stepper ol {
  display:flex;
  gap:10px;
  list-style:none;
  margin:0;
  padding:0;
  align-items:center
}

.stepper li {
  font-size:11px;
  display:flex;
  align-items:center;
  gap:7px
}

.stepper button {
  display:flex;
  align-items:center;
  gap:9px;
  border-radius:30px;
  padding:8px 14px;
  font-size:11px;
  transition:background .2s;
  white-space:nowrap
}

.stepper button b {
  font-size:11px;
  font-weight:500
}

.stepper button[aria-current],.stepper li[aria-current] {
  background:var(--ink);
  color:var(--paper)
}

.stepper button[aria-current] b {
  color:var(--lime)
}

.stepper button:disabled {
  color:#6e7068;
  opacity:1
}

.stepper button:not(:disabled):hover {
  background:var(--lime);
  color:var(--ink)
}

.stepper-note {
  font-size:8px;
  letter-spacing:1.6px;
  padding:0 18px;
  color:var(--muted)
}

.stepper li[aria-current] {
  padding:8px 20px;
  border-radius:30px
}

.scene {
  display:grid;
  grid-template-columns:repeat(12,minmax(0,1fr));
  gap:24px;
  padding:40px 0 32px;
  position:relative;
  min-height:620px;
  isolation:isolate
}

.scene-copy {
  grid-column:span 5;
  position:relative;
  z-index:2
}

.eyebrow,.tiny-label {
  font-size:9px;
  letter-spacing:1.8px;
  font-weight:650;
  display:block;
  line-height:1.6
}

.eyebrow {
  margin-bottom:24px
}

.status-dot {
  display:inline-block;
  width:6px;
  height:6px;
  border-radius:50%;
  background:var(--ink);
  margin-right:9px;
  vertical-align:1px
}

.display {
  font-size:clamp(65px,8.4vw,140px);
  font-weight:850;
  letter-spacing:-.085em;
  line-height:.87;
  margin:0 0 25px;
  outline:none
}

.display.medium {
  font-size:clamp(58px,6.8vw,112px)
}

.outline {
  -webkit-text-stroke:1.5px var(--ink);
  color:transparent
}

.display em {
  font-style:normal;
  background:var(--lime);
  padding:0 .06em .04em;
  display:inline-block;
  transform:rotate(-3deg);
  margin-top:8px
}

.intro-text {
  font-size:clamp(15px,1.5vw,21px);
  line-height:1.4;
  letter-spacing:-.4px;
  margin:24px 0;
  color:#41463f
}

.nepali-note {
  display:flex;
  align-items:center;
  gap:9px;
  transform:rotate(-6deg);
  margin:24px 0 25px 65px;
  font-family:Noto,Inter,sans-serif;
  font-weight:700;
  font-size:20px
}

.nepali-note svg {
  width:75px;
  height:40px;
  stroke:var(--ink);
  stroke-width:2;
  fill:none
}

.color-selection {
  margin-top:32px
}

.color-selection .eyebrow {
  font-size:8px;
  margin-bottom:7px
}

.color-selection h2 {
  font-size:21px;
  letter-spacing:-.6px;
  margin-bottom:16px
}

.swatches {
  display:flex;
  gap:11px
}

.swatch {
  width:34px;
  height:34px;
  padding:4px;
  border:1px solid transparent;
  border-radius:50%;
  transition:transform .25s,border-color .25s
}

.swatch span {
  display:block;
  width:100%;
  height:100%;
  background:var(--swatch);
  border-radius:50%;
  border:1px solid rgba(0,0,0,.12)
}

.swatch[aria-pressed=true] {
  border-color:var(--ink);
  transform:scale(1.08)
}

.swatch:hover {
  transform:translateY(-3px)
}

.wardrobe-panel {
  grid-column:span 7;
  position:relative;
  min-width:0;
  padding-top:4px
}

.wardrobe-top {
  display:flex;
  justify-content:space-between;
  align-items:flex-start;
  padding:0 18px 40px
}

.wardrobe-top .tiny-label {
  padding-top:12px;
  font-size:8px
}

.edition-tag {
  font-size:8px;
  line-height:1.4;
  padding:10px;
  border:1px solid var(--ink);
  border-radius:50%;
  height:60px;
  width:60px;
  text-align:center;
  display:flex;
  align-items:center;
  justify-content:center;
  transform:rotate(12deg)
}

.rail-line {
  position:absolute;
  top:106px;
  left:-20px;
  right:calc(-1 * var(--page-gutter));
  height:5px;
  border-radius:10px;
  background:linear-gradient(#7f807b,#c2c3ba,#686b62);
  box-shadow:0 3px 4px #0001
}

.wardrobe {
  overflow:hidden;
  position:relative;
  min-height:430px;
  touch-action:pan-y;
  cursor:grab;
  user-select:none;
  overscroll-behavior-x:contain;
  outline-offset:0;
  margin-right:calc(-1 * var(--page-gutter));
  padding-top:4px;
  margin-top:-2px
}

.wardrobe.dragging {
  cursor:grabbing
}

.rail-track {
  display:flex;
  gap:0;
  will-change:transform;
  width:max-content;
  align-items:flex-start;
  padding-left:0
}

.garment {
  flex:none;
  width:clamp(255px,28vw,440px);
  padding:0;
  position:relative;
  transform-origin:50% 0;
  transition:opacity .25s;
  touch-action:pan-y;
  margin-right:-72px;
  outline-offset:-8px
}

.garment .shirt-svg {
  filter:drop-shadow(10px 17px 11px #19201928);
  transform-origin:50% 5%;
  width:100%;
  will-change:transform;
  pointer-events:none
}

.garment:not(.focused) {
  opacity:.75
}

.garment.focused {
  z-index:2
}

.garment-label {
  display:flex;
  align-items:center;
  justify-content:center;
  gap:12px;
  font-size:9px;
  letter-spacing:1px;
  text-transform:uppercase;
  opacity:0;
  transition:opacity .3s;
  margin-top:-15px
}

.garment.focused .garment-label {
  opacity:1
}

.garment-label b {
  border:1px solid var(--ink);
  border-radius:50%;
  width:25px;
  height:25px;
  display:grid;
  place-items:center;
  font-weight:500
}

.orbit-sticker {
  position:absolute;
  right:0;
  bottom:57px;
  transform:rotate(12deg);
  width:125px;
  height:125px;
  border-radius:50%;
  background:var(--lime);
  display:flex;
  align-items:center;
  justify-content:center;
  z-index:3;
  box-shadow:3px 6px 0 #1a1a1a10;
  border:1px solid var(--ink);
  pointer-events:none
}

.orbit-sticker span {
  font-size:10px;
  line-height:1.15;
  text-align:center
}

.orbit-sticker strong {
  font-size:15px;
  line-height:.98;
  display:inline-block;
  margin-top:3px
}

.orbit-sticker i {
  position:absolute;
  left:12px;
  top:48px;
  font-size:25px;
  font-style:normal
}

.rail-bottom {
  display:flex;
  justify-content:space-between;
  gap:10px;
  align-items:center;
  margin-top:0;
  padding:0 10px
}

.rail-bottom>span {
  font-size:8px;
  letter-spacing:1.4px
}

.rail-bottom>div {
  display:flex;
  gap:8px
}

.icon-button {
  width:32px;
  height:32px;
  border:1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius:50%;
  font-size:17px
}

.icon-button:hover {
  background:var(--lime)
}

.journey-dock {
  display:flex;
  gap:24px;
  align-items:center;
  padding:12px 16px 12px 24px;
  position:sticky;
  bottom:18px;
  z-index:14;
  border-radius:18px;
  margin:0 0 34px;
  min-height:82px
}

.dock-caption {
  display:flex;
  flex-direction:column;
  gap:3px
}

.dock-caption span,.dock-price small {
  font-size:8px;
  letter-spacing:1.3px;
  color:var(--muted)
}

.dock-caption strong {
  font-size:14px;
  letter-spacing:-.3px
}

.dock-price {
  margin-left:auto;
  text-align:right;
  display:flex;
  flex-direction:column
}

.dock-price strong {
  font-size:19px;
  letter-spacing:-.8px
}

.button {
  border-radius:10px;
  display:inline-flex;
  justify-content:space-between;
  align-items:center;
  gap:28px;
  padding:17px 23px;
  font-size:12px;
  font-weight:600;
  line-height:1.25;
  transition:transform .2s,background .2s,box-shadow .2s;
  min-height:50px
}

.button>span {
  font-size:23px;
  line-height:1
}

.button.dark {
  background:var(--ink);
  color:var(--paper)
}

.button.dark:hover {
  background:#303728;
  box-shadow:4px 4px 0 var(--lime);
  transform:translate(-2px,-2px)
}

.button:active {
  transform:translateY(1px)
}

.button:disabled {
  opacity:.55;
  transform:none;
  box-shadow:none
}

.full-width {
  width:100%
}

.back-button {
  font-size:11px;
  padding:10px 18px 10px 0;
  border-right:1px solid var(--line)
}

.text-button {
  font-size:12px;
  font-weight:600;
  padding:14px 0;
  display:inline-block;
  text-decoration:underline;
  text-underline-offset:4px
}

.fine-print {
  font-size:10px;
  line-height:1.7;
  color:var(--muted);
  margin:13px 0
}

.ticker {
  overflow:hidden;
  border-top:1px solid var(--line);
  border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);
  transform:rotate(-1.5deg);
  width:102%;
  margin-left:-1%;
  background:var(--lime);
  margin-top:25px
}

.ticker>div {
  padding:10px 0;
  font-size:clamp(20px,2.6vw,40px);
  font-weight:800;
  letter-spacing:-1.4px;
  white-space:nowrap;
  animation:ticker 36s linear infinite;
  width:max-content
}

.site-footer {
  padding:52px var(--page-gutter) 30px;
  display:flex;
  justify-content:space-between;
  gap:24px;
  align-items:center;
  font-size:9px
}

.site-footer>span {
  font-family:Noto,Inter,sans-serif;
  font-weight:700
}

.site-footer>span span {
  font-size:8px;
  margin-left:8px;
  letter-spacing:1px
}

.site-footer>div {
  display:flex;
  gap:24px
}

.site-footer small {
  font-size:7px;
  letter-spacing:1px;
  color:var(--muted)
}

.print-scene .scene-copy {
  grid-column:span 6
}

.composition-panel {
  grid-column:span 6;
  position:relative;
  min-width:0
}

.preview-caption {
  display:flex;
  justify-content:space-between;
  font-size:8px;
  letter-spacing:1px;
  padding:0 16px
}

.shirt-stage {
  position:relative;
  display:flex;
  align-items:center;
  justify-content:center;
  max-height:490px
}

.shirt-stage canvas {
  position:relative;
  width:min(100%,440px);
  height:auto;
  z-index:1;
  filter:drop-shadow(15px 18px 10px #0002)
}

.stage-ring {
  position:absolute;
  width:87%;
  aspect-ratio:1;
  border-radius:50%;
  border:1px solid #17191b18;
  top:6%;
  background:radial-gradient(ellipse,#e9eddf66,transparent 70%)
}

.small-sticker {
  position:absolute;
  top:70px;
  right:0;
  background:var(--coral);
  border:1px solid var(--ink);
  border-radius:50%;
  width:82px;
  height:82px;
  display:grid;
  place-content:center;
  line-height:1;
  font-size:14px;
  font-weight:750;
  transform:rotate(13deg);
  z-index:2
}

.print-deck {
  display:grid;
  grid-template-columns:repeat(4,minmax(0,1fr));
  gap:0;
  margin:42px 0 26px;
  position:relative;
  padding:0 8px
}

.print-card {
  background:#fcfaf4;
  border:1px solid #d1d1c8;
  border-radius:7px;
  box-shadow:4px 8px 14px #0001;
  padding:10px;
  min-width:0;
  transform:rotate(var(--tilt));
  transition:transform .25s,box-shadow .25s;
  position:relative;
  margin:0 -2px
}

.print-card:hover,.print-card[aria-pressed=true] {
  transform:translateY(-15px) rotate(0);
  z-index:3;
  box-shadow:4px 8px 0 #17191b1c;
  border-color:var(--ink)
}

.print-card[aria-pressed=true]::after {
  content:'↗';
  position:absolute;
  bottom:-10px;
  right:-5px;
  border-radius:50%;
  background:var(--lime);
  border:1px solid var(--ink);
  height:25px;
  width:25px;
  font-size:16px
}

.print-card img {
  width:100%;
  aspect-ratio:1;
  object-fit:contain;
  pointer-events:none;
  margin:12px 0
}

.print-index {
  display:flex;
  justify-content:space-between;
  font-size:8px;
  font-weight:650;
  gap:4px
}

.print-index small {
  font-size:6px;
  letter-spacing:.2px;
  color:var(--muted)
}

.print-name {
  display:block;
  font-size:8px;
  font-weight:550;
  text-align:left;
  line-height:1.4;
  min-height:24px
}

.placement-controls {
  padding:18px 22px;
  position:relative;
  z-index:4;
  margin-top:-15px
}

.placement-controls legend {
  font-size:10px;
  font-weight:600;
  margin-bottom:9px
}

.segmented {
  display:flex;
  gap:4px;
  padding:4px;
  background:#17191b08;
  border-radius:9px
}

.segmented button {
  flex:1;
  font-size:11px;
  padding:8px;
  border-radius:6px
}

.segmented button[aria-pressed=true] {
  background:var(--ink);
  color:var(--paper)
}

.scale-control {
  display:block;
  margin-top:16px
}

.scale-control>span {
  display:flex;
  justify-content:space-between;
  font-size:10px
}

.scale-control output {
  font-size:10px;
  font-weight:650
}

.scale-control input {
  display:block;
  width:100%;
  accent-color:var(--ink);
  margin:12px 0 0;
  cursor:ew-resize
}

.reveal-scene .scene-copy {
  grid-column:span 5
}

.reveal-art {
  grid-column:span 7;
  position:relative;
  display:flex;
  flex-direction:column;
  align-items:center;
  perspective:1200px
}

.reveal-canvas-wrap {
  width:min(90%,465px);
  z-index:2;
  transform-style:preserve-3d
}

.reveal-canvas-wrap canvas {
  width:100%;
  height:auto;
  filter:drop-shadow(20px 25px 14px #0003)
}

.reveal-star {
  position:absolute;
  top:-50px;
  left:20px;
  right:0;
  text-align:center;
  font-size:490px;
  color:var(--lime);
  line-height:1;
  transform:rotate(15deg);
  z-index:0
}

.reveal-tools {
  display:flex;
  flex-direction:column;
  align-items:flex-start;
  gap:8px;
  margin-top:34px
}

.design-ticket {
  width:min(90%,480px);
  display:flex;
  align-items:center;
  justify-content:space-between;
  padding:22px;
  z-index:3;
  transform:rotate(-3deg);
  margin-top:-10px
}

.design-ticket small {
  font-size:7px;
  letter-spacing:1px;
  display:block
}

.design-ticket strong {
  font-size:13px;
  display:block;
  margin-top:5px
}

.design-ticket>span {
  font-size:9px;
  max-width:130px;
  line-height:1.4
}

.design-ticket i {
  font-size:27px;
  font-style:normal
}

.details-scene {
  align-items:start;
  grid-template-rows:auto 1fr
}

.details-heading {
  grid-column:1/-1;
  margin-bottom:10px
}

.details-heading .display {
  font-size:clamp(54px,6vw,88px)
}

.checkout-form {
  grid-column:span 8;
  padding:32px
}

.checkout-summary {
  grid-column:span 4;
  padding:24px;
  position:sticky;
  top:92px
}

.checkout-summary>img {
  aspect-ratio:5/6;
  object-fit:contain;
  border-radius:10px;
  background:#e8e6da;
  width:100%;
  margin-bottom:18px
}

.checkout-summary h2 {
  font-size:20px
}

.checkout-summary>p {
  font-size:11px;
  margin-top:8px
}

.checkout-summary dl {
  margin:22px 0 0;
  font-size:11px
}

.checkout-summary dl>div {
  display:flex;
  justify-content:space-between;
  gap:10px;
  padding:7px 0
}

.checkout-summary dd {
  margin:0;
  font-weight:600
}

.checkout-summary .total {
  border-top:1px solid var(--line);
  margin-top:10px;
  padding-top:16px;
  font-size:17px
}

.checkout-form fieldset {
  margin-bottom:30px
}

.checkout-form legend {
  font-size:17px;
  font-weight:600;
  letter-spacing:-.5px;
  margin-bottom:20px;
  width:100%;
  padding-bottom:12px;
  border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)
}

.checkout-form legend>span {
  font-size:10px;
  font-weight:500;
  border:1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius:50%;
  width:26px;
  height:26px;
  display:inline-grid;
  place-items:center;
  margin-right:9px
}

.form-grid {
  display:grid;
  grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;
  gap:18px
}

.field.wide {
  grid-column:1/-1
}

.field label,.upload-field {
  font-size:11px;
  font-weight:600;
  display:block;
  margin-bottom:7px
}

.field label small,.upload-field small {
  display:inline-block;
  font-size:9px;
  font-weight:400;
  color:var(--muted);
  margin-left:8px
}

.field {
  min-width:0
}

.field input:not([type=checkbox]),.field select,.field textarea,.upload-field input {
  width:100%;
  padding:12px 13px;
  border:1px solid #a6aaa0;
  border-radius:7px;
  background:#ffffff8c;
  font-size:13px;
  min-height:44px;
  line-height:1.4
}

.field select {
  appearance:auto
}

.field textarea {
  resize:vertical
}

.field input:focus,.field select:focus,.field textarea:focus {
  outline:2px solid var(--cobalt);
  outline-offset:2px;
  background:#fff
}

.field input[aria-invalid=true] {
  border-color:#a32821;
  outline-color:#a32821
}

.field input[type=file] {
  font-size:10px
}

.size-note {
  font-size:11px;
  padding-top:14px
}

.size-note summary {
  cursor:pointer
}

.size-note p {
  margin-top:10px;
  color:var(--muted)
}

.payment-tabs,.provider-options {
  display:flex;
  gap:14px;
  flex-wrap:wrap
}

.payment-tabs>label {
  display:flex;
  align-items:center;
  gap:8px;
  font-size:12px;
  border:1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius:8px;
  padding:12px;
  background:#fff5;
  cursor:pointer
}

.payment-tabs>label:has(input:disabled) {
  opacity:.55;
  cursor:not-allowed
}

.provider-options {
  margin:20px 0 10px
}

.provider-options label {
  font-size:12px;
  display:flex;
  align-items:center;
  gap:7px
}

.payment-info {
  display:flex;
  gap:18px;
  align-items:center;
  background:#ffffff7d;
  padding:15px;
  border-radius:10px;
  margin:14px 0
}

.payment-info img {
  width:140px;
  object-fit:contain
}

.payment-info p {
  font-size:12px;
  margin-top:6px
}

.upload-field {
  margin-top:20px
}

.upload-field input {
  display:block;
  margin-top:9px
}

.consent {
  display:flex;
  gap:10px;
  align-items:flex-start;
  font-size:10px;
  line-height:1.6;
  margin:15px 0 22px
}

.consent input {
  margin:2px 0 0
}

.consent input.input {
  width:18px
}

.honeypot {
  position:absolute;
  left:-9999px;
  width:1px;
  height:1px;
  overflow:hidden
}

.form-errors,.errorlist {
  background:#ffe5e0;
  color:#8a2119;
  font-size:12px;
  border-radius:7px;
  padding:14px 18px;
  margin:0 0 20px;
  list-style:none
}

.form-errors strong {
  display:block;
  margin-bottom:8px
}

.form-errors ul {
  margin:0
}

.errorlist {
  margin-top:8px
}

.success-message {
  background:#dff0c2;
  border:1px solid #92ad64;
  padding:14px;
  border-radius:8px;
  font-size:12px;
  margin:15px 0
}

.message {
  padding:18px;
  margin:20px var(--page-gutter)
}

.editorial-heading {
  display:flex;
  align-items:flex-end;
  gap:45px;
  justify-content:space-between;
  position:relative;
  padding:52px 0 42px
}

.editorial-heading .display {
  margin-bottom:0
}

.editorial-heading>p {
  font-size:15px;
  line-height:1.6;
  margin-bottom:10px;
  max-width:300px
}

.heading-sticker {
  background:var(--coral);
  width:90px;
  height:90px;
  display:grid;
  place-content:center;
  text-align:center;
  border-radius:50%;
  font-size:16px;
  font-weight:800;
  line-height:1;
  transform:rotate(15deg);
  flex:none
}

.catalogue-filter {
  display:flex;
  align-items:center;
  gap:18px;
  margin:0 0 24px;
  border-top:1px solid var(--line);
  padding-top:20px;
  font-size:11px
}

.catalogue-filter select {
  border:1px solid var(--line);
  background:#fff7;
  border-radius:8px;
  padding:9px 14px;
  min-width:150px
}

.product-grid {
  display:grid;
  grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr));
  gap:36px 26px
}

.product-card {
  min-width:0
}

.product-image {
  position:relative;
  border-radius:12px;
  overflow:hidden;
  background:#e6e5de
}

.product-image>img {
  width:100%;
  aspect-ratio:4/4.6;
  object-fit:cover;
  transition:transform .5s
}

.product-card:hover .product-image>img {
  transform:scale(1.035)
}

.product-category {
  position:absolute;
  top:14px;
  left:14px;
  padding:6px 12px;
  font-size:9px;
  border-radius:20px
}

.demo-badge {
  position:absolute;
  bottom:14px;
  right:14px;
  font-size:8px;
  letter-spacing:1px;
  background:var(--paper);
  color:var(--ink);
  padding:5px 8px;
  border-radius:3px
}

.product-title {
  display:flex;
  justify-content:space-between;
  gap:12px;
  align-items:center;
  margin:19px 0 8px
}

.product-title h2 {
  font-size:18px;
  letter-spacing:-.7px
}

.product-title strong {
  font-size:11px;
  white-space:nowrap
}

.product-card>p {
  font-size:10px;
  color:var(--muted);
  margin-bottom:12px
}

.product-links {
  display:flex;
  justify-content:space-between;
  font-size:11px;
  font-weight:600;
  gap:10px
}

.product-links>a:hover {
  text-decoration:underline
}

.pagination {
  display:flex;
  gap:30px;
  justify-content:center;
  font-size:12px;
  padding:35px 0
}

.empty-state {
  padding:48px;
  grid-column:1/-1;
  margin:50px 0;
  max-width:760px
}

.empty-state h1,.empty-state h2 {
  font-size:38px;
  margin-bottom:20px
}

.empty-state p {
  margin-bottom:24px
}

.review-score {
  padding:22px 28px;
  transform:rotate(4deg);
  min-width:210px
}

.review-score>strong {
  font-size:60px;
  letter-spacing:-5px;
  line-height:1;
  display:block
}

.review-score small {
  font-size:17px;
  letter-spacing:-1px;
  margin-left:5px
}

.review-score>span {
  display:block;
  font-size:11px;
  margin:12px 0
}

.review-score>a {
  font-size:11px;
  text-decoration:underline;
  text-underline-offset:4px
}

.reviews-layout {
  display:grid;
  grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1.8fr) minmax(300px,1fr);
  gap:32px;
  align-items:start;
  margin-top:20px
}

.review-wall {
  columns:2;
  column-gap:20px
}

.review-card {
  break-inside:avoid;
  margin:0 0 22px;
  padding:22px
}

.review-card:nth-child(3n+1) {
  transform:rotate(-1.5deg);
  background:#e3e9d7b8
}

.review-card:nth-child(3n+2) {
  transform:rotate(1deg);
  background:#e6e0f1b8
}

.review-card-top {
  display:flex;
  justify-content:space-between;
  align-items:center;
  font-size:20px
}

.rating {
  font-size:17px;
  letter-spacing:1px
}

.review-card img {
  border-radius:8px;
  margin:17px 0;
  width:100%;
  max-height:350px;
  object-fit:cover
}

.review-card blockquote {
  margin:20px 0 25px;
  font-size:17px;
  line-height:1.5;
  letter-spacing:-.3px;
  overflow-wrap:anywhere
}

.review-card footer {
  font-size:11px;
  display:flex;
  flex-direction:column;
  gap:3px
}

.review-card footer span {
  font-size:10px;
  color:var(--muted)
}

.review-form {
  padding:28px
}

.review-form h2 {
  font-size:35px;
  margin-bottom:10px
}

.review-form>p:not(.eyebrow) {
  font-size:12px;
  margin-bottom:22px
}

.review-form .field {
  margin-bottom:16px
}

.first-review {
  column-span:all;
  padding:45px;
  background:#d5ff4566;
  min-height:540px;
  position:relative;
  overflow:hidden
}

.first-review h2 {
  font-size:clamp(38px,4vw,68px);
  line-height:.98;
  max-width:440px;
  position:relative
}

.first-review p {
  margin:24px 0;
  font-size:16px;
  position:relative
}

.first-review>a {
  font-size:12px;
  text-decoration:underline;
  position:relative
}

.giant-asterisk {
  display:block;
  font-size:120px;
  line-height:1;
  margin-bottom:20px;
  transform:rotate(15deg);
  color:var(--ink)
}

.done-layout {
  display:grid;
  grid-template-columns:1.1fr 1fr;
  gap:60px;
  padding:60px 0 30px;
  align-items:start
}

.confirmation-id {
  padding:20px 25px;
  display:flex;
  flex-direction:column;
  gap:7px;
  margin:28px 0
}

.confirmation-id small {
  font-size:8px;
  letter-spacing:1px
}

.confirmation-id strong {
  font-size:21px;
  letter-spacing:-.6px;
  overflow-wrap:anywhere
}

.confirmation-id span {
  font-size:11px;
  color:var(--muted)
}

.action-row {
  display:flex;
  flex-wrap:wrap;
  gap:12px;
  margin:25px 0
}

.receipt-card {
  padding:22px;
  transform:rotate(2deg);
  position:relative
}

.receipt-card>img {
  width:100%;
  border-radius:8px
}

.receipt-card h2 {
  font-size:20px;
  margin-top:20px
}

.receipt-card p {
  font-size:12px;
  margin:10px 0
}

.receipt-card a {
  font-size:11px;
  text-decoration:underline
}

.receipt-tag {
  position:absolute;
  top:28px;
  left:0;
  background:var(--lime);
  padding:10px 16px;
  font-size:8px;
  letter-spacing:1px;
  transform:rotate(-8deg);
  border:1px solid var(--ink)
}

.tracking-layout {
  display:grid;
  grid-template-columns:1.1fr 1fr;
  gap:70px;
  padding-top:70px;
  min-height:600px;
  align-items:start
}

.tracking-card {
  padding:35px;
  margin-top:35px
}

.tracking-card .field {
  margin-bottom:20px
}

.tracking-arrow {
  font-size:150px;
  line-height:1.2;
  display:block;
  color:var(--cobalt)
}

.tracking-result {
  border-top:1px solid var(--line);
  padding-top:24px;
  margin-top:24px
}

.tracking-result h2 {
  font-size:36px;
  margin:13px 0
}

.tracking-result p,.tracking-result a {
  font-size:12px
}

.tracking-result a {
  display:inline-block;
  text-decoration:underline;
  margin-top:13px
}

.dhaka-thread {
  display:block;
  flex:none;
  width:72px;
  height:18px;
  background:url('dhaka-thread.svg') center/72px 18px
}

.toast {
  position:fixed;
  left:50%;
  bottom:25px;
  transform:translate(-50%,20px);
  padding:15px 22px;
  font-size:12px;
  z-index:100;
  opacity:0;
  pointer-events:none;
  transition:opacity .2s,transform .2s;
  max-width:90vw;
  text-align:center
}

.toast.show {
  opacity:1;
  transform:translate(-50%,0)
}

#cursor {
  position:fixed;
  left:0;
  top:0;
  pointer-events:none;
  background:var(--lime);
  border:1px solid var(--ink);
  border-radius:50%;
  width:64px;
  height:64px;
  display:flex;
  align-items:center;
  justify-content:center;
  font-size:9px;
  letter-spacing:1px;
  z-index:90;
  opacity:0;
  transform:translate(-50%,-50%);
  transition:opacity .15s
}

#cursor.visible {
  opacity:1
}

@keyframes ticker {
  to {
    transform:translateX(-50%)
  }

}

@supports not ((backdrop-filter:blur(1px)) or (-webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(1px))) {
  .glass {
    background:#f8f7ef
  }

  .review-card:nth-child(n) {
    background:#eeece3
  }

}

@media(min-width:1500px) {
  .scene {
    min-height:720px;
    padding-top:55px
  }

  .wardrobe {
    min-height:490px
  }

  .rail-line {
    top:106px
  }

  .color-selection {
    margin-top:45px
  }

  .orbit-sticker {
    bottom:90px
  }

}

@media(max-width:1100px) {
  .stepper-note {
    display:none
  }

  .stepper {
    justify-content:center
  }

  .primary-nav {
    gap:24px
  }

  .scene {
    gap:16px;
    min-height:550px
  }

  .garment {
    width:310px
  }

  .wardrobe {
    min-height:375px
  }

  .orbit-sticker {
    width:105px;
    height:105px;
    bottom:53px;
    right:-8px
  }

  .scene-copy {
    grid-column:span 5
  }

  .nepali-note {
    margin-left:20px
  }

  .color-selection {
    margin-top:25px
  }

  .print-scene .scene-copy {
    grid-column:span 6
  }

  .print-deck {
    grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr));
    gap:15px;
    max-width:320px;
    margin-top:25px
  }

  .print-card img {
    max-height:100px
  }

  .print-card {
    margin:0
  }

  .print-card:hover,.print-card[aria-pressed=true] {
    transform:translateY(-6px) rotate(0)
  }

  .print-name {
    font-size:9px
  }

  .reveal-star {
    font-size:390px;
    top:0
  }

  .product-grid {
    grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr))
  }

  .heading-sticker {
    display:none
  }

  .review-wall {
    columns:1
  }

  .checkout-form {
    grid-column:span 7;
    padding:24px
  }

  .checkout-summary {
    grid-column:span 5;
    padding:20px
  }

  .form-grid {
    gap:16px
  }

  .site-footer small {
    display:none
  }

}

@media(max-width:760px) {
  :root {
    --page-gutter:20px
  }

  .site-header {
    min-height:83px;
    gap:14px;
    flex-wrap:wrap;
    align-content:center;
    padding:12px 0
  }

  .wordmark {
    font-size:33px;
    letter-spacing:-2px;
    padding-right:20px
  }

  .wordmark small {
    font-size:6px;
    letter-spacing:1.7px;
    margin-top:6px
  }

  .logo-star {
    font-size:23px
  }

  .primary-nav {
    gap:18px;
    margin-left:auto
  }

  .primary-nav a {
    font-size:10px;
    padding:10px 0
  }

  .primary-nav a span {
    display:none
  }

  .header-track {
    font-size:0;
    gap:0
  }

  .header-track span {
    font-size:21px
  }

  .demo-strip {
    font-size:7px;
    gap:9px;
    line-height:1.5;
    margin-top:9px
  }

  .demo-strip span {
    font-size:8px;
    letter-spacing:0;
    max-width:250px
  }

  .studio,.page-wrap {
    padding-top:17px
  }

  .stepper {
    padding:6px 5px;
    top:8px;
    min-height:45px;
    backdrop-filter:blur(15px)
  }

  .stepper ol {
    gap:0;
    justify-content:space-between;
    width:100%
  }

  .stepper li {
    font-size:9px;
    gap:4px
  }

  .stepper button {
    font-size:9px;
    gap:5px;
    padding:7px 9px
  }

  .stepper button b {
    font-size:9px
  }

  .stepper li[aria-current] {
    padding:6px 10px
  }

  .stepper ol>li>span {
    font-size:8px
  }

  .scene {
    display:flex;
    flex-direction:column;
    gap:0;
    padding:32px 0 18px;
    min-height:auto
  }

  .color-scene {
    position:relative;
    padding-top:29px
  }

  .scene-copy {
    width:100%
  }

  .display {
    font-size:clamp(64px,16vw,110px);
    margin-bottom:18px;
    letter-spacing:-.08em;
    line-height:.88
  }

  .display.medium {
    font-size:clamp(55px,13.5vw,90px)
  }

  .eyebrow {
    font-size:8px;
    margin-bottom:18px;
    letter-spacing:1.25px
  }

  .intro-text {
    font-size:14px;
    margin:15px 0;
    line-height:1.5
  }

  .color-scene .scene-copy {
    display:grid;
    grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;
    gap:0 10px
  }

  .color-scene .scene-copy>.eyebrow {
    grid-column:1/-1
  }

  .color-scene .display {
    grid-column:1/-1;
    font-size:clamp(70px,17.2vw,130px)
  }

  .color-scene .intro-text {
    margin:0;
    font-size:12px
  }

  .nepali-note {
    grid-column:2;
    grid-row:3;
    font-size:16px;
    margin:0 0 0 auto;
    gap:0;
    align-self:center
  }

  .nepali-note svg {
    width:44px;
    height:30px
  }

  .color-selection {
    grid-column:1/-1;
    display:flex;
    align-items:center;
    justify-content:space-between;
    gap:10px;
    margin-top:25px
  }

  .color-selection .eyebrow {
    display:none
  }

  .color-selection h2 {
    font-size:15px;
    margin:0
  }

  .swatches {
    gap:6px
  }

  .swatch {
    width:28px;
    height:28px;
    padding:3px
  }

  .wardrobe-panel {
    width:100%;
    padding-top:0;
    margin-top:27px
  }

  .wardrobe-top {
    padding:0 0 24px
  }

  .wardrobe-top .tiny-label {
    font-size:7px;
    padding:0
  }

  .edition-tag {
    display:none
  }

  .rail-line {
    top:38px;
    left:calc(-1 * var(--page-gutter));
    height:4px
  }

  .wardrobe {
    min-height:310px;
    margin-left:calc(-1 * var(--page-gutter));
    padding-top:0
  }

  .rail-track {
    padding-left:16px
  }

  .garment {
    width:275px;
    margin-right:-50px
  }

  .garment-label {
    font-size:8px;
    margin-top:-12px;
    gap:7px
  }

  .garment-label b {
    width:21px;
    height:21px
  }

  .orbit-sticker {
    width:90px;
    height:90px;
    bottom:55px;
    right:0
  }

  .orbit-sticker strong {
    font-size:12px
  }

  .orbit-sticker span {
    font-size:8px
  }

  .orbit-sticker i {
    font-size:19px;
    left:8px;
    top:35px
  }

  .rail-bottom {
    padding:0;
    margin-top:3px
  }

  .rail-bottom>span {
    font-size:7px
  }

  .icon-button {
    width:30px;
    height:30px
  }

  .journey-dock {
    gap:12px;
    bottom:10px;
    margin:10px -8px 23px;
    padding:10px 12px;
    min-height:71px;
    border-radius:14px
  }

  .dock-caption {
    display:none
  }

  .dock-price {
    margin-left:0;
    text-align:left;
    flex:1;
    white-space:nowrap
  }

  .dock-price small {
    font-size:6px;
    letter-spacing:.8px
  }

  .dock-price strong {
    font-size:16px
  }

  .journey-dock .button {
    font-size:11px;
    padding:14px 17px;
    gap:17px;
    min-height:45px
  }

  .back-button {
    font-size:0;
    padding:8px 11px 8px 2px
  }

  .back-button::before {
    content:'←';
    font-size:20px
  }

  .ticker {
    margin-top:10px
  }

  .site-footer {
    flex-wrap:wrap;
    padding-top:35px;
    font-size:8px;
    gap:22px
  }

  .site-footer>div {
    gap:15px
  }

  .site-footer>span span {
    display:none
  }

  .print-scene .intro-text {
    font-size:13px
  }

  .print-deck {
    grid-template-columns:repeat(4,minmax(0,1fr));
    max-width:none;
    margin:28px 0 14px;
    gap:0;
    padding:0
  }

  .print-card {
    padding:7px;
    margin:0 -1px
  }

  .print-card img {
    margin:8px 0;
    height:auto;
    max-height:none
  }

  .print-index {
    font-size:6px
  }

  .print-index small {
    font-size:5px
  }

  .print-name {
    font-size:6px;
    min-height:20px
  }

  .print-card:hover,.print-card[aria-pressed=true] {
    transform:translateY(-7px) rotate(0)
  }

  .composition-panel {
    margin-top:14px;
    width:100%
  }

  .preview-caption {
    font-size:7px;
    padding:0 10px
  }

  .shirt-stage {
    max-height:380px
  }

  .shirt-stage canvas {
    width:340px;
    max-width:100%
  }

  .small-sticker {
    width:65px;
    height:65px;
    font-size:11px;
    top:45px;
    right:15px
  }

  .placement-controls {
    padding:15px 18px;
    margin-top:-5px
  }

  .reveal-scene .scene-copy {
    position:relative
  }

  .reveal-scene .display {
    max-width:80%;
    font-size:clamp(60px,15vw,95px)
  }

  .reveal-scene .intro-text {
    font-size:13px
  }

  .reveal-tools {
    flex-direction:row;
    flex-wrap:wrap;
    gap:12px;
    margin-top:20px;
    align-items:center
  }

  .reveal-tools .button {
    font-size:10px;
    padding:12px 15px;
    gap:13px
  }

  .reveal-tools .text-button {
    font-size:10px
  }

  .reveal-art {
    width:100%;
    margin-top:10px;
    overflow:hidden;
    padding-bottom:18px
  }

  .reveal-star {
    font-size:330px;
    top:0;
    left:0
  }

  .reveal-canvas-wrap {
    max-width:350px
  }

  .design-ticket {
    padding:15px;
    gap:10px;
    margin-top:-10px
  }

  .design-ticket>span {
    font-size:8px;
    max-width:100px
  }

  .details-heading .display {
    font-size:60px
  }

  .checkout-form {
    padding:22px 18px;
    order:3;
    width:100%
  }

  .checkout-summary {
    position:relative;
    top:0;
    order:2;
    margin:5px 0 24px;
    padding:18px;
    width:100%;
    display:grid;
    grid-template-columns:90px 1fr;
    gap:8px 16px
  }

  .checkout-summary>.eyebrow {
    grid-column:1/-1;
    margin:0
  }

  .checkout-summary>img {
    grid-column:1;
    grid-row:2/6;
    width:90px;
    align-self:center;
    margin:0
  }

  .checkout-summary h2 {
    grid-column:2;
    font-size:16px;
    margin-top:6px
  }

  .checkout-summary>p {
    grid-column:2;
    font-size:10px;
    margin:0
  }

  .checkout-summary>dl {
    grid-column:2;
    margin:2px 0 0;
    font-size:10px
  }

  .checkout-summary dl>div {
    padding:3px 0
  }

  .checkout-summary .total {
    margin:5px 0 0;
    padding-top:7px;
    font-size:14px
  }

  .checkout-summary>.fine-print {
    grid-column:1/-1;
    font-size:8px;
    margin-top:6px
  }

  .checkout-summary>.text-button {
    grid-column:1/-1;
    padding:8px 0 0;
    font-size:10px
  }

  .checkout-form legend {
    font-size:14px;
    margin-bottom:16px
  }

  .form-grid {
    gap:16px 12px
  }

  .field input:not([type=checkbox]),.field select,.field textarea {
    font-size:16px;
    padding:11px 10px
  }

  .field label {
    font-size:10px
  }

  .field label small {
    font-size:8px;
    margin-left:4px
  }

  .field:has(input[name=full_name]),.field:has(input[name=phone]),.field:has(input[name=municipality]) {
    grid-column:1/-1
  }

  .payment-tabs {
    gap:8px
  }

  .payment-tabs>label {
    font-size:10px;
    padding:10px
  }

  .payment-info {
    align-items:flex-start
  }

  .payment-info img {
    width:100px
  }

  .payment-info>div {
    min-width:0;
    overflow-wrap:anywhere
  }

  .payment-info p {
    font-size:10px
  }

  .consent {
    font-size:10px
  }

  .details-scene~.journey-dock {
    margin-top:10px
  }

  .editorial-heading {
    padding:32px 0 30px;
    gap:25px;
    align-items:flex-start;
    flex-direction:column
  }

  .editorial-heading>p {
    font-size:13px;
    max-width:none;
    margin:0
  }

  .catalogue-filter {
    gap:12px;
    flex-wrap:wrap;
    font-size:10px
  }

  .catalogue-filter label {
    width:100%;
    font-size:10px
  }

  .catalogue-filter select {
    flex:1;
    font-size:12px
  }

  .product-grid {
    gap:30px 14px
  }

  .product-category {
    padding:4px 7px;
    top:8px;
    left:8px;
    font-size:7px
  }

  .demo-badge {
    font-size:6px;
    padding:4px 6px;
    bottom:8px;
    right:8px;
    letter-spacing:.4px
  }

  .product-title {
    display:block;
    margin-top:14px
  }

  .product-title h2 {
    font-size:15px;
    line-height:1.2;
    margin-bottom:6px
  }

  .product-title strong {
    font-size:10px
  }

  .product-card>p {
    font-size:9px;
    line-height:1.6
  }

  .product-links {
    font-size:10px;
    flex-direction:column
  }

  .product-image {
    border-radius:8px
  }

  .review-score {
    min-width:0;
    width:100%;
    display:flex;
    align-items:center;
    gap:20px;
    transform:rotate(-2deg);
    padding:16px 20px
  }

  .review-score>strong {
    font-size:40px;
    letter-spacing:-3px
  }

  .review-score small {
    font-size:13px
  }

  .review-score>span {
    font-size:9px;
    max-width:90px;
    line-height:1.3;
    margin:0
  }

  .review-score>a {
    font-size:9px;
    margin-left:auto
  }

  .reviews-layout {
    grid-template-columns:1fr;
    gap:25px;
    margin-top:8px
  }

  .review-wall {
    columns:1
  }

  .first-review {
    padding:30px;
    min-height:0
  }

  .first-review h2 {
    font-size:45px;
    line-height:.95
  }

  .first-review p {
    font-size:14px
  }

  .giant-asterisk {
    font-size:75px
  }

  .review-form {
    padding:24px
  }

  .done-layout {
    grid-template-columns:1fr;
    gap:35px;
    padding:35px 0 0
  }

  .confirmation-id {
    padding:18px
  }

  .confirmation-id strong {
    font-size:18px
  }

  .done-layout .action-row {
    gap:10px
  }

  .done-layout .button {
    font-size:10px;
    padding:14px 17px;
    gap:14px
  }

  .receipt-card {
    padding:18px;
    transform:rotate(1deg)
  }

  .receipt-card h2 {
    font-size:18px
  }

  .tracking-layout {
    grid-template-columns:1fr;
    gap:20px;
    padding-top:40px;
    min-height:0
  }

  .tracking-arrow {
    display:none
  }

  .tracking-card {
    margin:0;
    padding:25px
  }

  .tracking-result h2 {
    font-size:30px
  }

  .toast {
    font-size:11px;
    width:max-content;
    max-width:90vw
  }

  .empty-state {
    padding:30px;
    margin:30px 0
  }

  .empty-state h1,.empty-state h2 {
    font-size:30px
  }

}

@media(max-width:370px) {
  .primary-nav {
    gap:12px
  }

  .primary-nav a {
    font-size:9px
  }

  .header-track {
    display:none
  }

  .stepper button {
    padding:6px 7px;
    font-size:8px;
    gap:4px
  }

  .color-selection h2 {
    font-size:13px
  }

  .swatches {
    gap:4px
  }

  .swatch {
    width:26px
  }

  .print-name {
    font-size:6px
  }

  .form-grid {
    grid-template-columns:1fr
  }

  .field.wide {
    grid-column:auto
  }

  .journey-dock .button {
    font-size:10px;
    padding:12px
  }

  .dock-price strong {
    font-size:14px
  }

}

@media(hover:none),(pointer:coarse) {
  #cursor {
    display:none
  }

  .print-card:hover {
    transform:rotate(var(--tilt))
  }

  .print-card[aria-pressed=true] {
    transform:translateY(-7px) rotate(0)
  }

}

@media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce) {
  html {
    scroll-behavior:auto
  }

  *,*::before,*::after {
    animation:none!important;
    transition:none!important;
    scroll-behavior:auto!important
  }

  #cursor {
    display:none
  }

  .garment .shirt-svg {
    will-change:auto
  }

  .rail-track {
    will-change:auto
  }

}
```

### studio/static/studio/vendor/gsap-README.md


````markdown
# GSAP (GreenSock Animation Platform)

[![GSAP - Animate anything](https://gsap.com/GSAP-share-image.png)](http://gsap.com)

GSAP is a **framework-agnostic** JavaScript animation library that turns developers into animation superheroes. Build high-performance animations that work in **every** major browser. Animate CSS, SVG, canvas, React, Vue, WebGL, colors, strings, motion paths, generic objects...anything JavaScript can touch! GSAP's <a href="https://gsap.com/docs/v3/Plugins/ScrollTrigger/">ScrollTrigger</a> plugin delivers jaw-dropping scroll-based animations with minimal code. <a href="https://gsap.com/docs/v3/GSAP/gsap.matchMedia()">gsap.matchMedia()</a> makes building responsive, accessibility-friendly animations a breeze.

No other library delivers such advanced sequencing, reliability, and tight control while solving real-world problems on over 12 million sites. GSAP works around countless browser inconsistencies; your animations ***just work***. At its core, GSAP is a high-speed property manipulator, updating values over time with extreme accuracy. It's up to 20x faster than jQuery!

GSAP is completely flexible; sprinkle it wherever you want. **Zero dependencies.**

There are many optional <a href="https://gsap.com/docs/v3/Plugins">plugins</a> and <a href="https://gsap.com/docs/v3/Eases">easing</a> functions for achieving advanced effects easily like <a href="https://gsap.com/docs/v3/Plugins/ScrollTrigger/">scrolling</a>, <a href="https://gsap.com/docs/v3/Plugins/MorphSVGPlugin">morphing</a>, [text splitting](https://gsap.com/docs/v3/Plugins/SplitText), animating along a <a href="https://gsap.com/docs/v3/Plugins/MotionPathPlugin">motion path</a> or <a href="https://gsap.com/docs/v3/Plugins/Flip/">FLIP</a> animations. There's even a handy <a href="https://gsap.com/docs/v3/Plugins/Observer/">Observer</a> for normalizing event detection across browsers/devices. 


### Get Started

[![Get Started with GSAP](http://gsap.com/_img/github/get-started.jpg)](http://gsap.com/get-started)


## Docs &amp; Installation

View the <a href="https://gsap.com/docs">full documentation here</a>, including an <a href="https://gsap.com/install">installation guide</a>.

### CDN

```html
<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/gsap@3.13/dist/gsap.min.js"></script>
```

See <a href="https://www.jsdelivr.com/gsap">JSDelivr's dedicated GSAP page</a> for quick CDN links to the core files/plugins. There are more <a href="https://gsap.com/install">installation instructions</a> at gsap.com.

**Every major ad network excludes GSAP from file size calculations** and most have it on their own CDNs, so contact them for the appropriate URL(s). 

### NPM
See the <a href="https://gsap.com/install">guide to using GSAP via NPM here</a>.

```javascript
npm install gsap
```

GSAP's core can animate almost anything including CSS and attributes, plus it includes all of the <a href="https://gsap.com/docs/v3/GSAP/UtilityMethods">utility methods</a> like <a href="https://gsap.com/docs/v3/GSAP/UtilityMethods/interpolate()">interpolate()</a>, <a href="https://gsap.com/docs/v3/GSAP/UtilityMethods/mapRange()">mapRange()</a>, most of the <a href="https://gsap.com/docs/v3/Eases">eases</a>, and it can do snapping and modifiers. 

```javascript
// typical import
import gsap from "gsap";

// get other plugins:
import ScrollTrigger from "gsap/ScrollTrigger";
import Flip from "gsap/Flip";
import Draggable from "gsap/Draggable";

// or all tools are exported from the "all" file (excluding members-only plugins):
import { gsap, ScrollTrigger, Draggable, MotionPathPlugin } from "gsap/all";

// don't forget to register plugins
gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger, Draggable, Flip, MotionPathPlugin); 
```

The NPM files are ES modules, but there's also a /dist/ directory with <a href="https://www.davidbcalhoun.com/2014/what-is-amd-commonjs-and-umd/">UMD</a> files for extra compatibility.

## GSAP is FREE! 

Thanks to [Webflow](https://webflow.com), GSAP is now **100% FREE** including ALL of the bonus plugins like [SplitText](https://gsap.com/docs/v3/Plugins/SplitText), [MorphSVG](https://gsap.com/docs/v3/Plugins/MorphSVGPlugin), and all the others that were exclusively available to Club GSAP members. That's right - the entire GSAP toolset is FREE, even for commercial use! 🤯  Read more [here](https://webflow.com/blog/gsap-becomes-free)

### ScrollTrigger &amp; ScrollSmoother

If you're looking for scroll-driven animations, GSAP's <a href="https://gsap.com/docs/v3/Plugins/ScrollTrigger/">ScrollTrigger</a> plugin is the standard. There's a companion <a href="https://gsap.com/docs/v3/Plugins/ScrollSmoother/">ScrollSmoother</a> as well.

[![ScrollTrigger](http://gsap.com/_img/github/scrolltrigger.jpg)](https://gsap.com/docs/v3/Plugins/ScrollTrigger)

### Using React? 

There's a <a href="https://www.npmjs.com/package/@gsap/react">@gsap/react</a> package that exposes a `useGSAP()` hook which is a drop-in replacement for `useEffect()`/`useLayoutEffect()`, automating cleanup tasks. Please read the <a href="https://gsap.com/react">React guide</a> for details.

### Resources

* <a href="https://gsap.com/">gsap.com</a>
* <a href="https://gsap.com/get-started/">Getting started guide</a>
* <a href="https://gsap.com/docs/">Docs</a>
* <a href="https://gsap.com/demos">Demos &amp; starter templates</a>
* <a href="https://gsap.com/community/">Community forums</a>
* <a href="https://gsap.com/docs/v3/Eases">Ease Visualizer</a>
* <a href="https://gsap.com/showcase">Showcase</a>
* <a href="https://www.youtube.com/@GreenSockLearning">YouTube Channel</a>
* <a href="https://gsap.com/cheatsheet">Cheat sheet</a>
* <a href="https://webflow.com">Webflow</a>

### Need help?
Ask in the friendly <a href="https://gsap.com/community/">GSAP forums</a>. Or share your knowledge and help someone else - it's a great way to sharpen your skills! Report any bugs there too (or <a href="https://github.com/greensock/GSAP/issues">file an issue here</a> if you prefer).

### License
GreenSock's standard "no charge" license can be viewed at <a href="https://gsap.com/standard-license">https://gsap.com/standard-license</a>.

Copyright (c) 2008-2025, GreenSock. All rights reserved.
````
